## lib/mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,38 @@
+import {finiteData as data,finiteFields as fields} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+import {finitePublicProfileTemplate} from './mcp-qa-finite-profile.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite connection profile unavailable');};
+const canonical=v=>JSON.stringify(v,(_,x)=>x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x)?Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(x).sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:1)):x);
+const id=x=>typeof x==='string'&&x.length>0&&x.length<=200&&!/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(x);
+/** Exact preparation grammar. Private source independently pins actual values;
+ * grammar/profile hashes are facts, never approval or provider authority. */
+export function qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile(input){
+ const {phase,metadata}=fields(input,['phase','metadata']);
+ if(!['closed_ui','read_consent','notice_consent'].includes(phase))fail();
+ const value=data(metadata),base=finitePublicProfileTemplate('canaryLive');
+ fields(value,Object.keys(base));
+ for(const k of Object.keys(base).filter(k=>k!=='bindings'))if(canonical(value[k])!==canonical(base[k]))fail();
+ const extras=['ACCESS_AUD','ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN','AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT','DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER','AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_POLICY'];
+ const names=[...base.bindings.map(b=>b.name),...extras];
+ if(!Array.isArray(value.bindings)||value.bindings.length!==names.length||new Set(value.bindings.map(b=>b.name)).size!==names.length||value.bindings.some(b=>!names.includes(b.name)))fail();
+ const binding=name=>value.bindings.find(b=>b.name===name),text=name=>{const b=fields(binding(name),['name','type','text']);if(b.type!=='plain_text'||typeof b.text!=='string')fail();return b.text;};
+ for(const rule of base.bindings){
+  const actual=binding(rule.name);
+  if(rule.name.endsWith('_ENABLED')){
+   const expected=rule.name==='AFW_DELEGATED_OAUTH_ENABLED'&&phase!=='closed_ui'||rule.name==='AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_ENABLED'&&phase==='notice_consent'?'true':'false';
+   if(text(rule.name)!==expected)fail();
+  }else if(rule.privateSlot){
+   const v=text(rule.name);
+   if(['AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_PROJECT_ID','AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_CLIENT_ID'].includes(rule.name)?!id(v):v!=='')fail();
+  }else if(canonical(actual)!==canonical(rule))fail();
+ }
+ if(!id(text('ACCESS_AUD'))||!/^[a-z0-9-]+\.cloudflareaccess\.com$/.test(text('ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN')))fail();
+ const expiry=text('AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT');if(!Number.isSafeInteger(Date.parse(expiry))||new Date(Date.parse(expiry)).toISOString()!==expiry)fail();
+ const limiter=fields(binding('DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER'),['name','type','namespace_id','simple']),simple=fields(limiter.simple,['limit','period']);
+ if(limiter.type!=='ratelimit'||typeof limiter.namespace_id!=='string'||!/^\d+$/.test(limiter.namespace_id)||!Number.isSafeInteger(simple.limit)||simple.limit<1||![10,60].includes(simple.period))fail();
+ const raw=text('AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_POLICY');
+ if(phase!=='notice_consent'){if(raw!=='')fail();}else{
+  if(raw.length>2048)fail();let policy;try{policy=fields(JSON.parse(raw),['grantId','projectId','clientId','startAt','expiresAt']);}catch{fail();}
+  if(!id(policy.grantId)||policy.projectId!==text('AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_PROJECT_ID')||policy.clientId!==text('AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_CLIENT_ID')||!Number.isSafeInteger(policy.startAt)||policy.startAt<0||!Number.isSafeInteger(policy.expiresAt)||policy.expiresAt<=policy.startAt||policy.expiresAt-policy.startAt>600000||policy.expiresAt>Date.parse(expiry))fail();
+ }
+ return value;
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-custody.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-custody.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-custody.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,27 @@
+import receipt from '../scripts/mcp-qa-finite-custody-receipt.json' with {type:'json'};
+import {finiteHash,finiteSame} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite custody unavailable');};
+const ACCOUNT='85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec';
+const approved=Object.freeze({id:receipt.tokenId,expiresAt:Date.parse(receipt.expiresAt),policyHash:receipt.policyHash,permissionGroupIds:Object.freeze([...receipt.permissionGroupIds])});
+/** Dated SOURCE commitment, NOT fresh policy equivalence. No key/raw policy. */
+export function finiteGhaCustody(){return approved;}
+export function assertFiniteGhaCustody(value){
+ if(!value||Object.keys(value).length!==4||value.id!==approved.id||value.expiresAt!==approved.expiresAt||value.policyHash!==approved.policyHash||!Array.isArray(value.permissionGroupIds)||!finiteSame([...value.permissionGroupIds].sort(),[...approved.permissionGroupIds].sort()))fail();
+ return approved;
+}
+/** Safe actual CF envelope, including informational code10000/type:null. */
+export function finiteCfEnvelope(value){
+ if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||Array.isArray(value)||Object.keys(value).some(k=>!['success','errors','messages','result','result_info'].includes(k))||value.success!==true||!Array.isArray(value.errors)||value.errors.length||!Array.isArray(value.messages)||!Object.hasOwn(value,'result'))fail();
+ for(const m of value.messages){
+  if(!m||typeof m!=='object'||Array.isArray(m)||Object.keys(m).some(k=>!['code','message','type'].includes(k))||!Number.isSafeInteger(m.code)||typeof m.message!=='string'||m.message.length>4096||m.type!==undefined&&m.type!==null&&typeof m.type!=='string')fail();
+ }
+ return value.result;
+}
+export async function verifyFiniteGhaCustody({custody,request,check,now,R1}){
+ const expected=assertFiniteGhaCustody(custody);check();
+ const r=await request('/accounts/'+ACCOUNT+'/tokens/verify');check();
+ const v=finiteCfEnvelope(r.value),expiry=Date.parse(v?.expires_on),notBefore=v?.not_before;
+ if(!v||Object.keys(v).some(k=>!['id','status','expires_on','not_before'].includes(k))||v.id!==expected.id||v.status!=='active'||expiry!==expected.expiresAt||expiry<R1||now()>=expiry||notBefore!==undefined&&notBefore!==null&&(!Number.isSafeInteger(Date.parse(notBefore))||Date.parse(notBefore)>now())||receipt.notBefore!==null&&Date.parse(notBefore)!==Date.parse(receipt.notBefore))fail();
+ check();
+ return Object.freeze({custodyHash:finiteHash({id:expected.id,expiresAt:expected.expiresAt,policyHash:expected.policyHash}),expiresAt:expected.expiresAt,scopeVerified:false,sourceQualification:receipt.sourceQualification});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-external-contract.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-external-contract.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-external-contract.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+import {finiteLauncherInstanceId,validateFiniteLaunchPolicy} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data,finiteHex as hex,finiteHash as hash,finiteTime as time,finiteSame as same} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+import {validateFiniteMaterialPreview,validateFiniteQaClaim,finiteObserverInstanceId} from './mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs';
+import {validateFiniteModule} from './mcp-finite-cleanup-policy.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite finalizer evidence unavailable');};
+export const FINITE_REPOSITORY='tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web';
+export const FINITE_QA='agent-friendly-web-independent-closure-qa';
+export const FINITE_ACCOUNT='85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec';
+export const finiteUuid=v=>typeof v==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(v);
+export function validateFiniteExternalPlan(value){
+ const p=fields(data(value),['purpose','preview','commandId','source','resources','deployments','canaryContainment','proofIdentity','launchPolicy','workflows','finalizer','custody']);
+ const v=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(p.preview);
+ if(p.purpose!=='afw.finite-finalizer-plan.v1'||!(/^[1-9][0-9]{0,19}$/).test(p.commandId))fail();
+ fields(p.source,['revision','tree','moduleHash','profileHash','bindingsHash']);
+ if(p.source.revision!==v.sourceRevision||!hex(p.source.tree,40)||['moduleHash','profileHash','bindingsHash'].some(k=>!hex(p.source[k])))fail();
+ fields(p.resources,['actorId','containerId','applicationId','namespaceId','qaD1Id','releaseD1Id','canaryActorId']);
+ if(p.resources.actorId!==v.actorId||['containerId','canaryActorId'].some(k=>!hex(p.resources[k]))||p.resources.applicationId!=='d6fc71552de7494a863aca20365a572d'||!hex(p.resources.namespaceId,32)||!finiteUuid(p.resources.qaD1Id)||!finiteUuid(p.resources.releaseD1Id)||p.resources.qaD1Id===p.resources.releaseD1Id)fail();
+ // The reference originates in the SAME native canonical control computation.
+ const resourceHash=hashResource(p.resources);if(resourceHash!==v.resourceHash)fail();
+ fields(p.deployments,['qaLive','qaClosed','canaryLive','canaryClosed','operatorClosed']);
+ for(const pin of Object.values(p.deployments)){fields(pin,['versionId','deploymentId','configurationHash','module']);validateFiniteModule(pin.module);if(!finiteUuid(pin.versionId)||!finiteUuid(pin.deploymentId)||!hex(pin.configurationHash))fail();}
+ if(p.deployments.qaLive.versionId!==v.runtimeVersionId||p.deployments.qaLive.module.sha256!==p.source.moduleHash||p.deployments.qaLive.versionId===p.deployments.qaClosed.versionId||p.deployments.canaryLive.versionId===p.deployments.canaryClosed.versionId)fail();
+ fields(p.canaryContainment,['sourceRevision','runtimeVersionId','moduleHash','profileHash','bindingsHash']);
+ if(!hex(p.canaryContainment.sourceRevision,40)||p.canaryContainment.runtimeVersionId!==p.deployments.canaryLive.versionId||p.canaryContainment.moduleHash!==p.deployments.canaryLive.module.sha256||['moduleHash','profileHash','bindingsHash'].some(k=>!hex(p.canaryContainment[k])))fail();
+ fields(p.proofIdentity,['trialId','grantHash','programHash','programSourceRevision']);if(['trialId','grantHash','programHash'].some(k=>!hex(p.proofIdentity[k]))||!hex(p.proofIdentity.programSourceRevision,40))fail();
+ validateFiniteLaunchPolicy({T0:v.T0,launchPolicy:p.launchPolicy});
+ fields(p.workflows,['confirmation','bootstrap','launcher']);
+ if(!/^afw-release-[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(p.workflows.confirmation)||p.workflows.bootstrap!==finiteObserverInstanceId({intentId:v.intentId,sourceRevision:v.sourceRevision})||p.workflows.launcher!==finiteLauncherInstanceId({intentId:v.intentId,sourceRevision:v.sourceRevision}))fail();
+ fields(p.finalizer,['repository','runId','attempt','jobId','workflow','sourceRevision','readyStep']);
+ if(p.finalizer.repository!==FINITE_REPOSITORY||p.finalizer.workflow!=='afw-finite-finalizer'||p.finalizer.sourceRevision!==v.sourceRevision||p.finalizer.attempt!==1||p.finalizer.readyStep!=='admit-and-ready'||!Number.isSafeInteger(p.finalizer.runId)||p.finalizer.runId<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(p.finalizer.jobId)||p.finalizer.jobId<1)fail();
+ fields(p.custody,['id','expiresAt','policyHash','permissionGroupIds']);
+ if(!hex(p.custody.id,32)||!time(p.custody.expiresAt)||p.custody.expiresAt<v.R1||!hex(p.custody.policyHash)||!Array.isArray(p.custody.permissionGroupIds)||p.custody.permissionGroupIds.length<1||p.custody.permissionGroupIds.length>16||p.custody.permissionGroupIds.some(x=>!hex(x,32))||new Set(p.custody.permissionGroupIds).size!==p.custody.permissionGroupIds.length)fail();
+ return p;
+}
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+function hashResource(value){return createHash('sha256').update('afw.qa-finite-resources.v1\n'+JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');}
+/** Native-only extraction: no raw selectors, consent, callback or legacy payload.
+ * Hashes are supplied by the control preview, never recomputed from a GHA plan. */
+export function projectFiniteExternalPlan({control,preview,custody}){
+ const p=control.plan,v=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(preview);
+ if(control.planHash!==v.planHash||control.controlHash!==v.controlHash||p.intentId!==v.intentId)fail();
+ return validateFiniteExternalPlan({purpose:'afw.finite-finalizer-plan.v1',preview:v,commandId:p.command.id,source:p.source,resources:p.resources,deployments:p.deployments,canaryContainment:p.canaryContainment,proofIdentity:{trialId:p.trialId,grantHash:p.grantHash,programHash:p.selectors.programHash,programSourceRevision:p.selectors.sourceRevision},launchPolicy:p.launchPolicy,workflows:p.workflows,finalizer:p.finalizer,custody});
+}
+export function validateFiniteObserverOutput(value,plan){
+ const p=validateFiniteExternalPlan(plan),v=p.preview,r=fields(data(value),['purpose','instanceId','provenance','preview','retained','completion','qaClaim','plan','primaryReceipt']);
+ if(!same(r.plan,p)||r.purpose!=='afw.finite-observer-output.v1'||r.instanceId!==p.workflows.bootstrap||!same(r.preview,v))fail();
+ const native=fields(r.provenance,['purpose','instanceId','sourceRevision','runtimeVersionId']);
+ if(native.purpose!=='afw.finite-observer-provenance.v1'||native.instanceId!==p.workflows.bootstrap||native.sourceRevision!==v.sourceRevision||native.runtimeVersionId!==v.runtimeVersionId)fail();
+ const retained=fields(r.retained,['purpose','intentId','controlHash','planHash','proofHash','capturedAt','proof','support']);
+ const {proofHash,...body}=retained;
+ if(retained.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-retained-proof.v1'||retained.intentId!==v.intentId||retained.controlHash!==v.controlHash||retained.planHash!==v.planHash||proofHash!==hash(body)||!time(retained.capturedAt)||retained.capturedAt<retained.proof.observedAt||retained.capturedAt-retained.proof.observedAt>5000||retained.capturedAt>=v.C1)fail();
+ const completion=fields(r.completion,['purpose','intentId','controlHash','proofHash','completedAt','canaryVersionId','canaryDeploymentId','readbackHash','effectReceipts','receiptHash']),{receiptHash,...completionBody}=completion;
+ if(completion.purpose!=='afw.finite-cleanup-completion.v1'||completion.intentId!==v.intentId||completion.controlHash!==v.controlHash||completion.proofHash!==proofHash||completion.canaryVersionId!==p.deployments.canaryClosed.versionId||!finiteUuid(completion.canaryDeploymentId)||receiptHash!==hash(completionBody)||!hex(completion.readbackHash)||!time(completion.completedAt)||completion.completedAt<retained.capturedAt||completion.completedAt>=v.C1)fail();
+ fields(completion.effectReceipts,['withdraw','close','seal','canary_restore']);if(Object.values(completion.effectReceipts).some(x=>!hex(x)))fail();
+ validateFiniteQaClaim(r.qaClaim,v,proofHash);
+ if(r.qaClaim.claim.issuedAt<completion.completedAt)fail();
+ validateFinitePrimaryReceipt(r.primaryReceipt,p,retained,completion,r.qaClaim);
+ validateProjectedFiniteCleanupProof({proof:retained.proof,support:retained.support,plan:p,expectedChallenge:retained.proof.proofChallenge,now:()=>retained.capturedAt});
+ return r;
+}
+import {validateProjectedFiniteCleanupProof} from './mcp-own-finite-cleanup-proof.mjs';
+export function validateFinitePrimaryReceipt(value,plan,retained,completion,qaClaim){
+ const p=validateFiniteExternalPlan(plan),v=p.preview,r=fields(data(value),['purpose','intentId','controlHash','planHash','proofHash','completionHash','nativeClaimHash','sealHash','sourceRevision','runtimeVersionId','actorId','closedCanaryVersionId','closedCanaryModuleHash','primary','writers','observedAt','receiptHash']),{receiptHash,...body}=r;
+ if(r.purpose!=='afw.finite-final-primary.v1'||r.intentId!==v.intentId||r.controlHash!==v.controlHash||r.planHash!==v.planHash||r.proofHash!==retained.proofHash||r.completionHash!==completion.receiptHash||r.nativeClaimHash!==qaClaim.claimHash||r.sealHash!==retained.proof.sealHash||r.sourceRevision!==v.sourceRevision||r.runtimeVersionId!==v.runtimeVersionId||r.actorId!==v.actorId||r.closedCanaryVersionId!==p.deployments.canaryClosed.versionId||r.closedCanaryModuleHash!==p.deployments.canaryClosed.module.sha256||!time(r.observedAt)||r.observedAt<qaClaim.claim.issuedAt||r.observedAt>=v.R1||receiptHash!==hash(body))fail();
+ const strip=x=>Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(x).filter(([k])=>k!=='observedAt'));
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-fixed-policy-engine.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-fixed-policy-engine.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-fixed-policy-engine.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+import {finiteCfEnvelope} from './mcp-qa-finite-custody.mjs';
+import {qualifyFiniteWorkerVersion} from './mcp-finite-primary-inventory.mjs';
+import {activationConfigurationHash,finiteActivationConfigurationHash} from './mcp-activation-observer.mjs';
+import {restorationConfigurationHash} from './mcp-restoration-configuration.mjs';
+import {retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise as retain} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+const ROOT='https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec/workers/',W='agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary',MAX=4718592;
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite policy transport unavailable');};
+const uuid=x=>typeof x==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(x);
+const current=lease=>{if(lease.signal.aborted)fail();lease.assertCurrent();};
+function configurationProjection(v){
+ return Object.fromEntries(['compatibility_date','compatibility_flags','main_module','exports','bindings','usage_model','containers'].map(k=>[k,v[k]]));
+}
+function configuration(v){
+ const c=configurationProjection(v);finiteActivationConfigurationHash(c);return data(c);
+}
+function historicalConfiguration(v){
+ const c=configurationProjection(v);activationConfigurationHash(c);return data(c);
+}
+
+/** Internal fixed provider operations. Admission and source remain with callers. */
+export function createFiniteFixedPolicyEngine({ctx,env},guard){
+ const storage=ctx.storage,db=env.RELEASE_DB,fetcher=globalThis.fetch,readToken=env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN,publishToken=env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN;
+ let failed=false;
+ const check=c=>{
+  if(failed)fail();
+  try{
+   guard();if(c)current(c);
+   if(ctx.storage!==storage||env.RELEASE_DB!==db||globalThis.fetch!==fetcher||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN!==readToken||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN!==publishToken)fail();
+  }catch(error){failed=true;throw error;}
+ };
+ const invoke=async(c,operation)=>{try{check(c);const value=await operation();check(c);return value;}catch(error){failed=true;throw error;}};
+ async function http(method,path,body,c){
+  check(c);const token=method==='GET'?readToken:publishToken;
+  if(typeof token!=='string'||!/^[-A-Za-z0-9_]{16,4096}$/.test(token)||typeof fetcher!=='function'){failed=true;fail();}
+  let rejectKnown;
+  const knownFailure=new Promise((_,reject)=>{rejectKnown=reject;});
+  // work, fetch, read and cancel are their actual native Promises, retained
+  // separately from the logical first failure. Cancellation is never drain.
+  const work=retain((async()=>{
+   let response,reader;
+   try{
+    response=await retain(fetcher(path,{method,redirect:'manual',cache:'no-store',signal:c.signal,headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+token,Accept:'application/json','Content-Type':'application/json','Cache-Control':'no-cache, no-store'},...(body?{body:JSON.stringify(body)}:{})}));
+    check(c);
+    if(!(response instanceof Response)||response.redirected||!(method==='GET'?response.status===200:[200,201].includes(response.status))||!response.body||!/^application\/json(?:\s*;|$)/i.test(response.headers.get('Content-Type')??''))fail();
+    const age=response.headers.get('Age'),cache=response.headers.get('CF-Cache-Status'),length=response.headers.get('Content-Length');
+    if(age!==null&&age!=='0'||cache!==null&&!['DYNAMIC','BYPASS','MISS'].includes(cache.toUpperCase())||length!==null&&(!/^\d+$/.test(length)||Number(length)>MAX))fail();
+    reader=response.body.getReader();const chunks=[];let size=0;
+    for(;;){const part=await retain(reader.read());check(c);if(part.done)break;size+=part.value.byteLength;if(size>MAX)fail();chunks.push(part.value);}
+    const raw=Buffer.concat(chunks,size);check(c);
+    return finiteCfEnvelope(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(raw)));
+   }catch(error){failed=true;rejectKnown(error);throw error;}
+   finally{if(reader)await retain(reader.cancel()).catch(()=>{});else if(response?.body&&!response.body.locked)await retain(response.body.cancel()).catch(()=>{});}
+  })());
+  return Promise.race([work,knownFailure]);
+ }
+ function pointer(value,versionId,deploymentId){
+  const deployment=value?.deployments?.[0];
+  if(!Array.isArray(value?.deployments)||value.deployments.length!==1||deployment?.id!==deploymentId||deployment.strategy!=='percentage'||!Array.isArray(deployment.versions)||deployment.versions.length!==1||deployment.versions[0].version_id!==versionId||deployment.versions[0].percentage!==100)fail();
+ }
+ function wire(config,baselineId,bytes){
+  if(!uuid(baselineId)||!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)||bytes.byteLength<1||bytes.byteLength>3145728)fail();
+  const value=configuration(config),out={...value,containers:value.containers.map(({class_name})=>({class_name})),bindings:value.bindings.map(b=>b.type==='secret_text'?{name:b.name,type:'inherit',version_id:baselineId}:b),modules:[{name:'index.mjs',content_type:'application/javascript+module',content_base64:Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64')}]};
+  for(const binding of out.bindings)if(binding.type==='inherit')fields(binding,['name','type','version_id']);
+  return out;
+ }
+ function describeUpload({configuration,baselineVersionId,bytes}){
+  check();
+  try{return Object.freeze({method:'POST',path:ROOT+'workers/'+W+'/versions?deploy=false',body:wire(configuration,baselineVersionId,bytes)});}
+  catch(error){failed=true;throw error;}
+ }
+ function describeActivation({versionId}){
+  check();if(!uuid(versionId)){failed=true;fail();}
+  return Object.freeze({method:'POST',path:ROOT+'scripts/'+W+'/deployments',body:{strategy:'percentage',versions:[{version_id:versionId,percentage:100}]}});
+ }
+ function result(value){
+  if(!uuid(value?.id))fail();
+  const reconciliation=value.exports_reconciliation;
+  if(reconciliation!==undefined&&(!reconciliation||Object.keys(reconciliation).some(k=>!['created','deleted','renamed','transfer_pending','transferred','updated','removable_entries','info','warnings'].includes(k))||Object.entries(reconciliation).some(([key,items])=>!Array.isArray(items)||(key==='updated'?items.some(x=>x!=='AfwOwnEventNode'):items.length>0))))fail();
+  return value;
+ }
+ return Object.freeze({
+  describeUpload,
+  upload(input,c){return invoke(c,async()=>{const request=describeUpload(input);return result(await http(request.method,request.path,request.body,c));});},
+  describeActivation,
+  activate(input,c){return invoke(c,async()=>{const request=describeActivation(input);return result(await http(request.method,request.path,request.body,c));});},
+  readPointer(pin,c){return invoke(c,async()=>{if(!uuid(pin.versionId)||!uuid(pin.deploymentId))fail();pointer(await http('GET',ROOT+'scripts/'+W+'/deployments?per_page=1',null,c),pin.versionId,pin.deploymentId);});},
+  readVersion(pin,c,closed=false){return invoke(c,async()=>{
+   if(!uuid(pin.versionId))fail();
+   const value=await http('GET',ROOT+'workers/'+W+'/versions/'+pin.versionId+'?include=modules',null,c);check(c);
+   qualifyFiniteWorkerVersion({metadata:value,pin:{versionId:pin.versionId,module:pin.module,configurationHash:restorationConfigurationHash(pin.configuration)},role:'canary',closed});
+   if(closed){if(activationConfigurationHash(historicalConfiguration(value))!==activationConfigurationHash(historicalConfiguration(pin.configuration)))fail();}
+   else if(finiteActivationConfigurationHash(configuration(value))!==finiteActivationConfigurationHash(configuration(pin.configuration)))fail();
+   return value;
+  });}
+ });
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+  }
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+  if(adoptionParent){
+   if(!['queued','running','waiting'].includes(r.status))fail();

+/** GitHub primary metadata plus append-only tag/ref fences; never update/delete.

+/** Fixed restore adapter. The returned readSnapshot closes over a separate

+export function createPrivateQaFiniteRestoreHttp({plan,credential,githubToken,fetchImpl=fetch,now=Date.now,signal}={}){
+ const p=validateFiniteExternalPlan(plan),v=p.preview;
+ const readWire=wire({credential,fetchImpl,now,T0:v.T0,R1:v.R1,signal});
+ const writeWire=wire({credential,fetchImpl,now,T0:v.T0,R1:v.R1,signal});

+   for(const part of ['schedules','tails']){const r=(await get('/workers/scripts/'+name+'/'+part,undefined,parent,assertCurrent)).value,a=Array.isArray(r)?r:r?.schedules;if(!Array.isArray(a)||a.length)fail();}
+  }
+  const exposure=(await get('/workers/scripts/'+FINITE_QA+'/subdomain',undefined,parent,assertCurrent)).value;
+  if(exposure?.enabled!==false||exposure.previews_enabled!==false)fail();
+  const namespaces=await get('/workers/durable_objects/namespaces',{page:1,per_page:1000},parent,assertCurrent),ni=namespaces.info;
+  if(!Array.isArray(namespaces.value)||namespaces.value.length>1000||ni?.page!==1||ni.per_page!==1000||ni.count!==namespaces.value.length||ni.total_count!==namespaces.value.length||ni.total_pages!==undefined&&ni.total_pages!==1)fail();
+  const ns=namespaces.value.filter(x=>x.script===FINITE_QA),expected={IndependentClosure:'070c4a7708eb4592a3ded7c117a88225',PrivateQaPreregistration:'6f30104244424152b800bc64d5f4cfac'};
+  if(ns.length!==2||new Set(ns.map(x=>x.id)).size!==2||ns.some(x=>x.id!==expected[x.class]||x.use_sqlite!==true))fail();
+  const qualifiedExecution={intentId:v.intentId,controlHash:v.controlHash,sourceRevision:v.sourceRevision,moduleHash:p.source.moduleHash,confirmationId:p.workflows.confirmation,observerId:p.workflows.bootstrap,launcherId:p.workflows.launcher,admissionHash:hash(p)};
+  const inventory=await readFiniteLaunchTrialAfwWorkflows({read:(path,query)=>get(path,query,parent,assertCurrent),assertCurrent,phase:'qa_restore',confirmationId:p.workflows.confirmation,observerId:p.workflows.bootstrap,launcherId:p.workflows.launcher,qualifiedExecution});
+  const rows=[],seen=new Set(),cursors=new Set();let cursor;
+  for(let page=0;page<4;page++){
+   const r=await get('/containers/applications/'+p.resources.applicationId+'/instances-v2',{per_page:100,...(cursor?{page_token:cursor}:{})},parent,assertCurrent);
+   if(!Array.isArray(r.value)||r.value.length>100||r.info?.per_page!==100||(r.info.page_token??'')!==(cursor??''))fail();
+   for(const row of r.value){
+    if(!hex(row.id)||seen.has(row.id)||row.application_id!==p.resources.applicationId||row.image!=='cloudflare/debian-trixie'||row.status?.state!=='stopped'||row.status.exit_code!==0)fail();seen.add(row.id);
+    const startedAt=Date.parse(row.started_at),stoppedAt=Date.parse(row.status.updated_at);
+    if(!time(startedAt)||!time(stoppedAt)||stoppedAt<startedAt||stoppedAt>now())fail();
+    rows.push({containerId:row.id,applicationId:row.application_id,image:row.image,startedAt,stoppedAt,state:'stopped',exitCode:0});
+   }
+   if(rows.length>64)fail();const next=r.info.next_page_token;
+   if(!next)break;if(page===3||typeof next!=='string'||next.length>1024||cursors.has(next))fail();cursors.add(next);cursor=next;
+  }
+  rows.sort((a,b)=>a.containerId.localeCompare(b.containerId));
+  if(!same(rows,output.retained.support.providerAfter.instances))fail();
+  const g=await github.inspect('restore',parent,assertCurrent);assertCurrent();
+  // Last pointer/observer read fences writes or replacement during qualification.
+  pointer((await get('/workers/scripts/'+FINITE_QA+'/deployments',undefined,parent,assertCurrent)).value,closed?p.deployments.qaClosed:p.deployments.qaLive,qa.id);
+  const after=await observer.read(parent,assertCurrent);assertCurrent();if(after.status!=='complete'||!same(after.output,observed.output)||now()<began)fail();
+  return data({purpose:'afw.finite-qa-snapshot.v1',observedAt:now(),qaVersionId:version,qaDeploymentId:qa.id,output,restoreJobId:g.restoreJobId,custodyExpiresAt:custody.expiresAt,profilesVerified:true,namespacesPreserved:true,exposureDisabled:true,providerSettled:true,workflowsTerminal:true,primarySealedChainVerified:true,githubVerified:true,evidenceHash:hash({qa,inventory,rows,outputHash:hash(output),github:g.evidenceHash,custodyHash:custody.custodyHash})});
+ }
+ async function deployClosed(input,parent,assertCurrent){
+  const x=fields(input,['record','recordHash']);if(sent||x.recordHash!==hash(x.record)||x.record.key!==finiteSendKey(p))fail();sent=true;github.consumeNewSend(x.record);
+  const check=()=>{assertCurrent();github.assertSendExecution(x.record);};check();

+  if(!finiteUuid(response.value?.id))fail();return {deploymentId:response.value.id};

+}
+
+/** Actual finalizer admit job identity from primary GitHub, before CF custody.
+ * Source constructor may use these technical facts, never caller actor/job DTO. */
+export async function readFiniteFinalizerGithubContext({admission,githubToken,fetchImpl=fetch,now=Date.now,signal}={}){
+ assertQaFiniteConfirmationAdmission(admission,'cleanup');
+ const client=wire({credential:githubToken,fetchImpl,now,T0:admission.createdAt,R1:admission.cleanupExpiresAt,signal});

+ const jobs=await get('/actions/runs/'+admission.runId+'/attempts/1/jobs?per_page=100'),found=jobs.jobs?.filter(x=>x.name==='admit');
+ if(!Array.isArray(jobs.jobs)||jobs.total_count!==jobs.jobs.length||jobs.jobs.length>100||found?.length!==1||!Number.isSafeInteger(found[0].id))fail();

+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {initialFields as fields} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {encodeFiniteInitialEntry,decodeFiniteInitialEntry} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry-records.mjs';
+import {sealFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as seal,encodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as encode,decodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as decode} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.mjs';
+import {qualifyFiniteInitialUploadedVersion} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs';
+
+// Pure unmounted data construction. No journal write, network call, live clock,
+// admission or eligibility is implied by the returned values.
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial activation wire unavailable');};
+const sha=value=>createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
+const record=(kind,value)=>decode(kind,encode(kind,value));
+const uuid=value=>typeof value==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(value);
+const time=value=>Number.isSafeInteger(value)&&value>=0;
+const path='/workers/scripts/agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary/deployments';
+function bodyFor(plan){
+ const annotation='afw-finite-initial-activate-'+plan.recordHash;
+ return {annotation,body:JSON.stringify({strategy:'percentage',versions:[{version_id:plan.candidateVersionId,percentage:100}],annotations:{'workers/message':annotation}})};
+}
+function checkedSend(plan,input){
+ const send=record('ActivationSend',input),{annotation,body}=bodyFor(plan);
+ if(send.planHash!==plan.recordHash||send.entryHash!==plan.entryHash||send.materialHash!==plan.materialHash||send.candidateVersionId!==plan.candidateVersionId||send.annotation!==annotation||send.bodyHash!==sha(body)||send.issuedAt<plan.I0||send.deadline>plan.forwardDeadline)fail();
+ return send;
+}
+function checkedAck(plan,send,input){
+ const ack=record('ActivationAck',input);
+ if(ack.planHash!==plan.recordHash||ack.sendHash!==send.recordHash||ack.deploymentId===plan.baselineDeploymentId||ack.acknowledgedAt<send.issuedAt||ack.acknowledgedAt>=send.deadline)fail();
+ return ack;
+}
+function pointer(plan,value,deploymentId){
+ const {annotation}=bodyFor(plan),v=value;
+ if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||!uuid(v.id)||v.id!==deploymentId||v.strategy!=='percentage'||!Array.isArray(v.versions)||v.versions.length!==1||v.versions[0].version_id!==plan.candidateVersionId||v.versions[0].percentage!==100||v.annotations?.['workers/message']!==annotation)fail();
+}
+export function prepareFiniteInitialActivationWire(input){
+ const v=fields(input,['plan','arm','entry','issuedAt','deadline']),plan=record('Plan',v.plan),arm=record('ArmAck',v.arm),entry=decodeFiniteInitialEntry(encodeFiniteInitialEntry(v.entry));
+ const state=seal('State',{planHash:plan.recordHash,deliveries:0,status:'pending',nextAt:plan.C0,previousHash:null});
+ if(entry.entryHash!==plan.entryHash||entry.I0!==plan.I0||entry.initialDeadline!==plan.initialDeadline||entry.operationDeadline!==plan.forwardDeadline||entry.sourceRevision!==plan.sourceRevision||entry.sourceTree!==plan.sourceTree||entry.qaVersionId!==plan.qaVersionId||entry.initialInstanceId!==plan.initialInstanceId||entry.commandId!==plan.commandId||arm.planHash!==plan.recordHash||arm.stateHash!==state.recordHash||arm.alarmAt!==plan.C0||arm.acknowledgedAt<plan.I0||arm.acknowledgedAt>=plan.forwardDeadline||!time(v.issuedAt)||!time(v.deadline)||v.issuedAt<arm.acknowledgedAt||v.deadline>plan.forwardDeadline)fail();
+ const {annotation,body}=bodyFor(plan);
+ const send=seal('ActivationSend',{planHash:plan.recordHash,entryHash:plan.entryHash,materialHash:plan.materialHash,candidateVersionId:plan.candidateVersionId,bodyHash:sha(body),annotation,issuedAt:v.issuedAt,deadline:v.deadline});
+ return Object.freeze({path,body,send,authorizesEffects:false});
+}
+export function qualifyFiniteInitialActivationAck(input){
+ const v=fields(input,['plan','send','result','acknowledgedAt']),plan=record('Plan',v.plan),send=checkedSend(plan,v.send);
+ if(!time(v.acknowledgedAt)||v.acknowledgedAt<send.issuedAt||v.acknowledgedAt>=send.deadline||v.result?.id===plan.baselineDeploymentId)fail();
+ pointer(plan,v.result,v.result?.id);
+ return seal('ActivationAck',{planHash:plan.recordHash,sendHash:send.recordHash,deploymentId:v.result.id,acknowledgedAt:v.acknowledgedAt});
+}
+export function qualifyFiniteInitialActivationCompletion(input){
+ const v=fields(input,['plan','send','ack','pointer','metadata','bytes','settledAt','observedAt']),plan=record('Plan',v.plan),send=checkedSend(plan,v.send),ack=checkedAck(plan,send,v.ack);
+ if(!time(v.settledAt)||!time(v.observedAt)||v.settledAt<ack.acknowledgedAt||v.observedAt<v.settledAt||v.observedAt>=send.deadline)fail();
+ pointer(plan,v.pointer,ack.deploymentId);
+ if(!(v.bytes instanceof Uint8Array)||v.bytes.length!==plan.moduleBytes||sha(v.bytes)!==plan.moduleHash)fail();
+ qualifyFiniteInitialUploadedVersion(v.metadata,plan.candidateVersionId,plan.configuration,v.bytes);
+ return seal('ActivationCompletion',{planHash:plan.recordHash,sendHash:send.recordHash,ackHash:ack.recordHash,candidateVersionId:plan.candidateVersionId,deploymentId:ack.deploymentId,settledAt:v.settledAt,observedAt:v.observedAt});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-anchors.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-anchors.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-anchors.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-anchors.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+import {retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise as retain} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+import {FINITE_INITIAL_CAPSULE_KEY as KEY,decodeFiniteInitialCapsule,sealFiniteInitialCapsule,encodeFiniteInitialCapsule} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-capsule-records.mjs';
+import {decodeFiniteInitialEntry} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry-records.mjs';
+import {decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as materialRecord,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHex as hex,initialFields as fields} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {FINITE_INITIAL_CLEANUP_PREFIX as P,FINITE_INITIAL_CLEANUP_KINDS as K,decodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as decode,sealFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as seal} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.mjs';
+// SAME compiler literals as the full QA readers; DATA descriptors only here.
+// Keeping their decoder/bootstrap modules out of the shared graph preserves M -> QA acyclicity.
+const compiledRevision=globalThis.__AFW_QA_SOURCE_REVISION__??'';
+const compiledTree=globalThis.__AFW_FINITE_SOURCE_TREE__??'';
+const compiledResidency=globalThis.__AFW_FINITE_CANDIDATE_RESIDENCY__??null;
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial anchors unavailable');};
+function descriptor(){
+ if(!hex(compiledRevision,40)||!hex(compiledTree,40))fail();
+ const e=fields(compiledResidency,['purpose','encoding','sourceRevision','sourceTree','sourceCheck','programHash','module','compressedSha256','compressedBytes','content']),moduleDescriptor=fields(e.module,['name','sha256','bytes']);
+ if(e.purpose!=='afw.finite-candidate-residency.v1'||e.encoding!=='gzip-base64.v1'||e.sourceCheck!=='git-inputs-before-after-v1'||e.sourceRevision!==compiledRevision||e.sourceTree!==compiledTree||!hex(e.programHash)||!hex(e.compressedSha256)||!Number.isSafeInteger(e.compressedBytes)||e.compressedBytes<1||e.compressedBytes>2098176||typeof e.content!=='string'||e.content.length!==4*Math.ceil(e.compressedBytes/3)||!/^(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4})*(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?$/.test(e.content)||moduleDescriptor.name!=='index.mjs'||!hex(moduleDescriptor.sha256)||!Number.isSafeInteger(moduleDescriptor.bytes)||moduleDescriptor.bytes<1||moduleDescriptor.bytes>2097152)fail();
+ // This comparison does NOT qualify bytes, custody or primary state. The real
+ // transport hashes actual baseline bytes and freshly qualifies its source;
+ // the existing QA initial/owner readers retain the full decoder/primary gates.
+ return Object.freeze({sourceRevision:compiledRevision,sourceTree:compiledTree,module:moduleDescriptor});
+}
+/** Fixed factual read only. The caller retains its existing request/alarm scope. */
+export async function readPrivateQaFiniteInitialAnchors({ctx,env},originalLease){
+ const storage=ctx?.storage,get=storage?.get,list=storage?.list,getAlarm=storage?.getAlarm,transaction=storage?.transaction,sync=storage?.sync;
+ const actor=ctx?.id,toString=actor?.toString,ns=env?.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,idFromName=ns?.idFromName,metadata=env?.AFW_QA_VERSION,qa=metadata?.id,db=env?.RELEASE_DB,reader=env?.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN,publisher=env?.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN,fetcher=globalThis.fetch;
+ if([get,list,getAlarm,transaction,sync,toString,idFromName].some(x=>typeof x!=='function'))fail();
+ const actorId=Reflect.apply(toString,actor,[]),resident=descriptor(),expected=resident;
+ const guard=()=>{originalLease.assertCurrent();if(originalLease.signal.aborted||ctx.storage!==storage||storage.get!==get||storage.list!==list||storage.getAlarm!==getAlarm||storage.transaction!==transaction||storage.sync!==sync||ctx.id!==actor||actor.toString!==toString||Reflect.apply(toString,actor,[])!==actorId||!hex(actorId)||env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE!==ns||ns.idFromName!==idFromName||Reflect.apply(idFromName,ns,['afw-own-successor-closure-v1']).toString()!==actorId||env.AFW_QA_VERSION!==metadata||metadata.id!==qa||env.RELEASE_DB!==db||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN!==reader||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN!==publisher||globalThis.fetch!==fetcher)fail();};
+ const io=async(method,args)=>{guard();const result=await retain(Reflect.apply(method,storage,args));guard();return result;};
+ const anchors=[],read=async k=>{const raw=await io(get,[k]);anchors.push(Object.freeze([k,raw]));return raw;};
+ const raw=await read(KEY),capsule=decodeFiniteInitialCapsule(raw);
+ if(capsule.sourceRevision!==expected.sourceRevision||capsule.sourceTree!==expected.sourceTree||capsule.qaVersionId!==qa||resident.sourceRevision!==capsule.sourceRevision||resident.sourceTree!==capsule.sourceTree||resident.module.sha256!==capsule.moduleHash)fail();
+ const prep='afw.finite-initial-preparation.v1:';
+ const entry=decodeFiniteInitialEntry(await read(prep+'entry')),claim=materialRecord('InitialClaim',await read(prep+'once')),selector=materialRecord('InitialSelector',await read(prep+'selector')),materialAck=materialRecord('InitialAck',await read(prep+'ack')),material=materialRecord('InitialMaterial',await read(prep+'material:'+capsule.materialHash));
+ const rows=await io(list,[{prefix:P,limit:16}]),records={};
+ if(rows.size>Object.keys(K).length)fail();
+ for(const [k,v]of rows){const name=k.slice(P.length);if(!Object.hasOwn(K,name))fail();records[name]=decode(K[name],v);anchors.push(Object.freeze([k,v]));}
+ const plan=records.plan,state=records.state;
+ if(!plan||!state||state.planHash!==plan.recordHash)fail();
+ const zero=seal('State',{planHash:plan.recordHash,deliveries:0,status:'pending',nextAt:plan.C0,previousHash:null});
+ const rebuilt=sealFiniteInitialCapsule({entry,claim,selector,materialAck,material,plan,state:zero,arm:records['arm-ack'],send:records['activation-send'],activationAck:records['activation-ack'],completion:records['activation-completion'],recordedAt:capsule.recordedAt});
+ if(encodeFiniteInitialCapsule(rebuilt)!==raw||plan.moduleHash!==resident.module.sha256||plan.moduleBytes!==resident.module.bytes)fail();
+ // Current recovery state is deliberately distinct from the immutable zero-state commitment.
+ if(state.nextAt!==(state.status==='complete'||state.deliveries===3?null:plan.C0+state.deliveries*60000)||state.deliveries===0&&canonical(state)!==canonical(zero))fail();
+ const alarm=await io(getAlarm,[]);
+ guard();return Object.freeze({capsule,entry,claim,selector,materialAck,material,plan,state,zero,records:Object.freeze(records),actorId,alarm,anchors:Object.freeze(anchors)});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-capsule-records.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-capsule-records.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-capsule-records.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-capsule-records.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex,encodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as encodeMaterial,decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as decodeMaterial,hashFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as materialHash} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {encodeFiniteInitialEntry,decodeFiniteInitialEntry} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-entry-records.mjs';
+import {encodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as encodeCleanup,decodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord as decodeCleanup} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-cleanup-records.mjs';
+import {prepareFiniteInitialActivationWire} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-activation-wire.mjs';
+const PURPOSE='afw.finite-initial-capsule.v1',CEILING=Date.parse('2026-11-03T02:59:00Z'),CAP=4096;
+export const FINITE_INITIAL_CAPSULE_KEY='afw.finite-initial-preparation.v1:capsule';
+const KEYS=['purpose','entryHash','materialHash','selectorHash','materialAckHash','cleanupPlanHash','cleanupArmAckHash','activationCompletionHash','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','qaDeploymentId','initialInstanceId','preconnectionId','I0','initialDeadline','C0','C1','activatedVersionId','activatedDeploymentId','moduleHash','configurationHash','bindingsHash','recordedAt'];
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial capsule unavailable');};
+const uuid=v=>typeof v==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(v);
+const time=v=>Number.isSafeInteger(v)&&v>=0&&v<=8640000000000000;
+function validate(input){
+ const v=own(fields(input,[...KEYS,'capsuleHash']));
+ if(v.purpose!==PURPOSE||Buffer.byteLength(canonical(v))>CAP)fail();
+ for(const k of KEYS.filter(k=>k.endsWith('Hash')))if(!hex(v[k]))fail();
+ if(!hex(v.capsuleHash)||!hex(v.sourceRevision,40)||!hex(v.sourceTree,40)||!hex(v.preconnectionId)||typeof v.initialInstanceId!=='string'||!/^afw-finite-initial-[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(v.initialInstanceId))fail();
+ if(!['qaVersionId','qaDeploymentId','activatedVersionId','activatedDeploymentId'].every(k=>uuid(v[k]))||v.activatedVersionId===v.qaVersionId)fail();
+ if(!['I0','initialDeadline','C0','C1','recordedAt'].every(k=>time(v[k]))||v.initialDeadline!==v.I0+300000||v.C0!==v.I0+900000||v.C1!==v.C0+180000||v.C1>CEILING||v.recordedAt<v.I0||v.recordedAt>=v.initialDeadline)fail();
+ if(v.capsuleHash!==hash(PURPOSE,Object.fromEntries(KEYS.map(k=>[k,v[k]]))))fail();
+ return v;
+}
+/** Complete M bindings commitment (expectedFull convention), not public projection. */
+export function finiteInitialCapsuleBindingsHash(input){
+ const bindings=own(input);
+ if(!Array.isArray(bindings)||bindings.length===0)fail();
+ const names=new Set();
+ for(const b of bindings){if(!b||typeof b.name!=='string'||!b.name||names.has(b.name))fail();names.add(b.name);}
+ return createHash('sha256').update(canonical([...bindings].sort((a,b)=>a.name<b.name?-1:a.name>b.name?1:0))).digest('hex');
+}
+/** Pure factual linkage only. No continuation, capability, persistence or effects. */
+export function sealFiniteInitialCapsule(input){
+ const f=fields(input,['entry','claim','selector','materialAck','material','plan','state','arm','send','activationAck','completion','recordedAt']);
+ const entry=decodeFiniteInitialEntry(encodeFiniteInitialEntry(f.entry));
+ const m=(kind,v)=>decodeMaterial(kind,encodeMaterial(kind,v)),c=(kind,v)=>decodeCleanup(kind,encodeCleanup(kind,v));
+ const claim=m('InitialClaim',f.claim),selector=m('InitialSelector',f.selector),ack=m('InitialAck',f.materialAck),material=m('InitialMaterial',f.material);
+ const plan=c('Plan',f.plan),state=c('State',f.state),arm=c('ArmAck',f.arm),send=c('ActivationSend',f.send),activationAck=c('ActivationAck',f.activationAck),completion=c('ActivationCompletion',f.completion);
+ const intent=material.intent,intentHash=hash('afw.finite-initial-intent.v1',intent),selectorHash=materialHash('InitialSelector',selector);
+ if(canonical(intent)!==canonical(claim.intent)||claim.commandHash!==entry.commandHash||claim.claimHash!==material.claimHash||claim.ids.preconnectionId!==material.preconnectionId)fail();
+ for(const k of ['commandId','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','initialInstanceId','I0','initialDeadline'])if(intent[k]!==entry[k]||plan[k]!==entry[k])fail();
+ const o=material.originalContext;
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex,initialInstanceId} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {QA_CLOUD_CLOSED_PINS} from './mcp-qa-cloud-closed-pins.mjs';
+import {restorationConfigurationHash} from './mcp-restoration-configuration.mjs';
+import {qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile} from './mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial cleanup record unavailable');};
+const uuid=v=>typeof v==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(v);
+const time=v=>Number.isSafeInteger(v)&&v>=0;
+export const FINITE_INITIAL_CLEANUP_PREFIX='afw.finite-initial-cleanup.v1:';
+export const FINITE_INITIAL_CLEANUP_KINDS=Object.freeze({plan:'Plan',state:'State','arm-ack':'ArmAck','activation-send':'ActivationSend','activation-ack':'ActivationAck','activation-completion':'ActivationCompletion','restore-send':'RestoreSend','restore-ack':'RestoreAck',receipt:'Receipt'});
+const schemas={
+ Plan:['entryHash','materialHash','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','initialInstanceId','commandId','I0','initialDeadline','forwardDeadline','C0','C1','candidateVersionId','moduleHash','moduleBytes','configuration','configurationHash','manifestHash','baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId','readerId','publisherId','readerExpiresAt','publisherExpiresAt'],
+ State:['planHash','deliveries','status','nextAt','previousHash'],
+ ArmAck:['planHash','stateHash','alarmAt','acknowledgedAt'],
+ ActivationSend:['planHash','entryHash','materialHash','candidateVersionId','bodyHash','annotation','issuedAt','deadline'],
+ ActivationAck:['planHash','sendHash','deploymentId','acknowledgedAt'],
+ ActivationCompletion:['planHash','sendHash','ackHash','candidateVersionId','deploymentId','settledAt','observedAt'],
+ RestoreSend:['planHash','activationCompletionHash','bodyHash','annotation','issuedAt','deadline'],
+ RestoreAck:['planHash','sendHash','deploymentId','acknowledgedAt'],
+ Receipt:['planHash','outcome','deploymentId','restoreAckHash','observedAt']
+};
+const purpose=k=>'afw.finite-initial-cleanup-'+k.toLowerCase()+'.v1';
+function validate(kind,input){
+ if(!Object.hasOwn(schemas,kind))fail();
+ const v=own(fields(input,['purpose',...schemas[kind],'recordHash'])),{recordHash,...body}=v;
+ if(v.purpose!==purpose(kind)||!hex(recordHash)||hash(v.purpose,body)!==recordHash)fail();
+ for(const k of ['entryHash','materialHash','planHash','moduleHash','configurationHash','manifestHash','stateHash','sendHash','ackHash','bodyHash','activationCompletionHash'])if(Object.hasOwn(v,k)&&!hex(v[k]))fail();
+ for(const k of ['qaVersionId','candidateVersionId','deploymentId','baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId'])if(Object.hasOwn(v,k)&&!uuid(v[k]))fail();
+ for(const k of ['I0','initialDeadline','forwardDeadline','C0','C1','readerExpiresAt','publisherExpiresAt','alarmAt','acknowledgedAt','issuedAt','deadline','settledAt','observedAt'])if(Object.hasOwn(v,k)&&!time(v[k]))fail();
+ if(kind==='Plan'){
+  const p=QA_CLOUD_CLOSED_PINS.canary;
+  if(!hex(v.sourceRevision,40)||!hex(v.sourceTree,40)||v.initialInstanceId!==initialInstanceId(v)||v.initialDeadline!==v.I0+300000||v.forwardDeadline>v.initialDeadline||v.forwardDeadline<=v.I0||v.C0!==v.I0+900000||v.C1!==v.C0+180000||v.readerExpiresAt<v.C1||v.publisherExpiresAt<v.C1||v.readerId!=='9aa97ad1fc9afe3021cf69ea497d5190'||v.publisherId!=='05718105dcdfc92e55cc3624172c2740'||v.baselineVersionId!==p.id||v.baselineDeploymentId!==p.deploymentId||[p.id,v.qaVersionId].includes(v.candidateVersionId)||!Number.isSafeInteger(v.moduleBytes)||v.moduleBytes<1||v.moduleBytes>2097152||restorationConfigurationHash(v.configuration)!==v.configurationHash)fail();
+  qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile({phase:'closed_ui',metadata:v.configuration});
+ }
+ if(kind==='State'&&(!Number.isSafeInteger(v.deliveries)||v.deliveries<0||v.deliveries>3||!['pending','processing','admin_pending','complete'].includes(v.status)||v.nextAt!==null&&!time(v.nextAt)||v.previousHash!==null&&!hex(v.previousHash)))fail();
+ if(kind==='Receipt'&&(!['never_activated','restored'].includes(v.outcome)||v.restoreAckHash!==null&&!hex(v.restoreAckHash)||v.outcome==='never_activated'&&v.restoreAckHash!==null||v.outcome==='restored'&&!hex(v.restoreAckHash)))fail();
+ if(kind.endsWith('Send')&&(typeof v.annotation!=='string'||v.annotation.length>128||v.deadline<=v.issuedAt||v.deadline-v.issuedAt>5000))fail();
+ if(kind==='ActivationCompletion'&&v.observedAt<v.settledAt)fail();
+ return v;
+}
+/** Recovery DATA codec; no activation producer, admission or persistence. */
+export function sealFiniteInitialCleanupRecord(kind,input){const body={purpose:purpose(kind),...own(input)};return validate(kind,{...body,recordHash:hash(body.purpose,body)});}
+export function encodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord(kind,input){return canonical(validate(kind,input));}
+export function decodeFiniteInitialCleanupRecord(kind,raw){if(typeof raw!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(raw)>65536)fail();const v=validate(kind,JSON.parse(raw));if(canonical(v)!==raw)fail();return v;}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+import {initialFields as fields} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite native custody observation unavailable');};

+const timestamp=value=>{
+ if(typeof value!=='string'||!/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\d(?:\.\d{3})?Z$/.test(value))fail();
+ const n=Date.parse(value);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||new Date(n).toISOString().replace('.000Z','Z')!==value.replace('.000Z','Z'))fail();return n;
+};
+/** Unmounted read-only observation of two loaded credential identities and
+ * one fixed historical Workflow. Never an admission, permission, or effect. */

+ let timer,onAbort,controller;
+ try{
+  fields(options,['ctx','env']);fields(originalLease,['signal','assertCurrent']);
+  const {ctx,env}=options,{signal,assertCurrent}=originalLease,actor=ctx?.id,toString=actor?.toString,self=env?.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,idFromName=self?.idFromName,reader=env?.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN,publisher=env?.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN,fetcher=globalThis.fetch;
+  if(!(signal instanceof AbortSignal)||typeof assertCurrent!=='function'||typeof toString!=='function'||typeof idFromName!=='function'||typeof fetcher!=='function'||[reader,publisher].some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||!/^[-A-Za-z0-9_]{16,4096}$/.test(x)))fail();

+  controller=new AbortController();
+  const stop=new Promise((_,reject)=>{onAbort=()=>{controller.abort();reject(Error('Finite native custody observation unavailable'));};signal.addEventListener('abort',onAbort,{once:true});timer=setTimeout(onAbort,15000);});
+  const check=()=>{
+   const at=Date.now(),m=performance.now();
+   if(signal.aborted||controller.signal.aborted||!Number.isSafeInteger(at)||at<last||at-wall>=15000||!Number.isFinite(m)||m<lastMono||m-mono>=15000||ctx.id!==actor||actor.toString!==toString||Reflect.apply(toString,actor,[])!==id||env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE!==self||self.idFromName!==idFromName||Reflect.apply(idFromName,self,[SLOT]).toString()!==id||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN!==reader||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_PUBLISH_TOKEN!==publisher||globalThis.fetch!==fetcher||originalLease.signal!==signal||originalLease.assertCurrent!==assertCurrent)fail();

+
+/** Requires the existing internal scope; this creates no admission or lease. */

+ try{
+  fields(options,['ctx','env']);fields(originalLease,['signal','assertCurrent']);

+ }catch{return unavailable();}
+}
+
+/** Fixed native actor pre-entry read. Internal retention is not an admission. */
+export async function readFinitePreEntryCustody(options){
+ let controller,timer;
+ try{
+  fields(options,['ctx','env']);
+  const {ctx,env}=options,storage=ctx.storage,db=env.RELEASE_DB;
+  const start=Date.now(),mono=performance.now();let last=start,lastMono=mono;

+  }});
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+import {initialFields as fields,initialOwn as own,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex,initialInstanceId} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+const PURPOSE='afw.finite-initial-entry.v1',CEILING=Date.parse('2026-11-03T02:59:00Z');
+const KEYS=['purpose','commandId','commandHash','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','initialInstanceId','workflowName','createdAt','I0','initialDeadline','operationDeadline'];
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial entry unavailable');};
+const time=x=>Number.isSafeInteger(x)&&x>=0&&x<=8640000000000000;
+function validate(input,complete){
+ const v=own(fields(input,[...KEYS,...complete?['entryHash']:[]]));
+ if(v.purpose!==PURPOSE||!Number.isSafeInteger(v.commandId)||v.commandId<1||!hex(v.commandHash)||!hex(v.sourceRevision,40)||!hex(v.sourceTree,40)||v.initialInstanceId!==initialInstanceId(v)||v.workflowName!=='afw-own-qa-private-bootstrap'||!['createdAt','I0','initialDeadline','operationDeadline'].every(k=>time(v[k]))||v.createdAt<v.I0||v.initialDeadline!==v.I0+300000||v.operationDeadline!==Math.min(v.initialDeadline,v.createdAt+30000,CEILING)||v.operationDeadline<=v.createdAt||Buffer.byteLength(canonical(v))>4096)fail();
+ if(complete&&(!hex(v.entryHash)||v.entryHash!==hash(PURPOSE,Object.fromEntries(KEYS.map(k=>[k,v[k]])))))fail();return v;
+}
+export function hashFiniteInitialEntry(value){return hash(PURPOSE,validate(value,false));}
+export function encodeFiniteInitialEntry(value){return canonical(validate(value,true));}
+export function decodeFiniteInitialEntry(raw){if(typeof raw!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(raw)>4096)fail();const v=validate(JSON.parse(raw),true);if(canonical(v)!==raw)fail();return v;}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+export const FINITE_INITIAL_PREPARATION_OPERATION='finite_initial_preparation';
+/** Real Workflow.run event; account/binding governance, not cryptographic RPC caller proof. */
+export async function runPrivateQaFiniteInitialPreparation({env,event,step}){
+ let parent;
+ try{
+  const timestamp=event.timestamp,createdAt=Reflect.apply(nativeTime,timestamp,[]),payload=event.payload,p=own(fields(payload,['operation','commandId','sourceRevision','qaVersionId'])),stepDo=step?.do,self=env?.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,idFromName=self?.idFromName,namespaceGet=self?.get,metadata=env?.AFW_QA_VERSION,qaVersionId=metadata?.id;
+  const r=refs({commandId:p.commandId,sourceRevision:p.sourceRevision,qaVersionId:p.qaVersionId,initialInstanceId:event.instanceId,workflowName:event.workflowName,createdAt});
+  if(p.operation!==FINITE_INITIAL_PREPARATION_OPERATION||event.schedule!==undefined||[stepDo,idFromName,namespaceGet].some(x=>typeof x!=='function')||qaVersionId!==r.qaVersionId||r.sourceRevision!==readFiniteQaBootstrapSourceExpectations().sourceRevision)fail();
+  const holders=()=>{if(event.timestamp!==timestamp||Reflect.apply(nativeTime,timestamp,[])!==createdAt||event.payload!==payload||canonical(own(fields(payload,['operation','commandId','sourceRevision','qaVersionId'])))!==canonical(p)||event.instanceId!==r.initialInstanceId||event.workflowName!==WORKFLOW||event.schedule!==undefined||step.do!==stepDo||env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE!==self||self.idFromName!==idFromName||self.get!==namespaceGet||env.AFW_QA_VERSION!==metadata||metadata.id!==qaVersionId)fail();};

+   current(parent.lease);
+   const actualRpc=Reflect.apply(method,actor,[r]),result=await parent.wait(actualRpc);
+   const data=nativeResult(result,r);current(parent.lease);return data;
+  }]);
+  const value=await parent.wait(actualStep),data=nativeResult(value,r);
+  current(parent.lease);return data;
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+    seen.add(row.name);
+   }
+   check();return Object.freeze({purpose:'afw.finite-initial-history-schema.v1',schemaVerified:true,triggerCount:seen.size,authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+  }));
+  const result=await Promise.race([actual,stopped]);check();return result;
+ }finally{active=false;clearTimeout(timer);signal.removeEventListener('abort',onAbort);}
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-lease.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-lease.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-lease.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-lease.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+// Internal initial-phase lease mechanism. Authority and absolute ceiling remain fixed by each caller.
+const current=(l,fail)=>{l.assertCurrent();if(l.signal.aborted)fail();};
+export function createFiniteInitialLease(deadline,ceiling,holders,fail){
+ const entered=Date.now(),mono=performance.now(),controller=new AbortController(),pendingWork=new Set();
+ let end=Math.min(deadline,ceiling),last=entered,lastMono=mono,closed=false,timer;
+ const stop=()=>controller.abort();
+ const check=()=>{holders();const at=Date.now(),m=performance.now();if(closed||controller.signal.aborted||!Number.isSafeInteger(at)||at<last||at>=end||!Number.isFinite(m)||m<lastMono||m-mono>=end-entered)fail();last=at;lastMono=m;return at;};
+ const lease=Object.freeze({signal:controller.signal,assertCurrent:check});
+ const retain=p=>{pendingWork.add(p);const settled=()=>pendingWork.delete(p);p.then(settled,settled);return p;};
+ const narrow=until=>{check();if(!Number.isSafeInteger(until))fail();end=Math.min(end,until);clearTimeout(timer);check();timer=setTimeout(stop,Math.max(1,end-Date.now()));};
+ const wait=async(p,l=lease)=>{let abort;try{return await Promise.race([retain(p),new Promise((_,reject)=>{abort=()=>reject(Error('Finite initial expired'));l.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});if(l.signal.aborted)abort();})]).then(v=>{current(l,fail);return v;});}finally{l.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abort);}};
+ const child=async work=>{
+  check();const began=Date.now(),m0=performance.now(),until=Math.min(end,began+5000),c=new AbortController();let alive=true,t;
+  const abort=()=>c.abort();controller.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});t=setTimeout(abort,Math.max(1,until-Date.now()));
+  const l=Object.freeze({signal:c.signal,assertCurrent(){check();if(!alive||c.signal.aborted||Date.now()>=until||performance.now()-m0>=until-began)fail();}});
+  let actual;
+  try{current(l,fail);actual=work(l,until);if(!actual||typeof actual.then!=='function')fail();retain(actual);return await wait(actual,l);}finally{alive=false;clearTimeout(t);controller.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abort);c.abort();}
+ };
+ narrow(end);return{lease,child,wait,narrow,retain,end(){check();return end;},close(){closed=true;clearTimeout(timer);stop();}};
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';

+export const initialHash=(domain,value)=>createHash('sha256').update(domain+'\n'+initialCanonical(value)).digest('hex');
+export const initialHex=(v,n=64)=>typeof v==='string'&&new RegExp('^[a-f0-9]{'+n+'}$').test(v);
+const uuid=v=>typeof v==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(v);
+const time=v=>Number.isSafeInteger(v)&&v>=0&&v<=8640000000000000;
+const id=v=>typeof v==='string'&&v.length>0&&v.length<=200&&!/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(v);
+const check=v=>{if(!v)fail();};
+export function initialFields(v,keys){return finiteFields(v,keys);}
+export function initialOwn(value){
+ let nodes=0;
+ const copy=(v,depth=0)=>{
+  if(++nodes>16384||depth>24)fail();
+  if(v===null||typeof v==='boolean'||typeof v==='number'&&Number.isSafeInteger(v))return v;
+  if(typeof v==='string'){if(Buffer.byteLength(v)>65536)fail();return v;}
+  const array=Array.isArray(v);

+  const out=array?[]:{};
+  for(const k of keys.filter(k=>!(array&&k==='length')).sort()){
+   const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v,k);
+   if(typeof k!=='string'||['__proto__','constructor','prototype','toJSON'].includes(k)||!d?.enumerable||!Object.hasOwn(d,'value')||array&&!/^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/.test(k))fail();
+   out[k]=copy(d.value,depth+1);
+  }
+  return Object.freeze(out);
+ };
+ const out=copy(value);if(Buffer.byteLength(initialCanonical(out))>65536)fail();return out;
+}
+export function initialInstanceId({commandId,sourceRevision,qaVersionId}){
+ check(Number.isSafeInteger(commandId)&&commandId>0&&initialHex(sourceRevision,40)&&uuid(qaVersionId));
+ return 'afw-finite-initial-'+initialHash('afw.finite-initial-instance.v1',{operation:'finite_initial_preparation',commandId,sourceRevision,qaVersionId});
+}
+function intent(input){
+ const v=initialFields(input,['purpose','repository','commandId','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','initialInstanceId','I0','initialDeadline','goal']);
+ check(v.purpose==='afw.finite-initial-intent.v1'&&v.repository==='tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web'&&initialHex(v.sourceRevision,40)&&initialHex(v.sourceTree,40)&&uuid(v.qaVersionId)&&time(v.I0)&&v.initialDeadline===v.I0+300000&&v.goal==='content'&&v.initialInstanceId===initialInstanceId(v));return v;
+}
+function ids(input){
+ const v=initialFields(input,['preconnectionId','trialId','activationKey','withdrawalRequestId','saveIdempotencyKey']);
+ check(['preconnectionId','trialId','activationKey'].every(k=>initialHex(v[k]))&&uuid(v.withdrawalRequestId)&&uuid(v.saveIdempotencyKey));return v;
+}
+export function validateFiniteInitialOriginalContext(input){
+ // Preserve Task4 v1: finiteData limits BEFORE its recursively sorted digest.
+ const v=finiteData(initialFields(input,['artifact','scope','original','publication','cancellations','retirementPolicy','withdrawalRequestId','saveIdempotencyKey','legacy','trialId']));
+ const a=initialFields(v.artifact,['sourceRevision','sourceTree','programHash','workerHash','sourceCheck']);
+ check(initialHex(a.sourceRevision,40)&&initialHex(a.sourceTree,40)&&initialHex(a.programHash)&&initialHex(a.workerHash)&&a.sourceCheck==='git-inputs-before-after-v1');
+ const s=initialFields(v.scope,['accountId','workerName','namespaceId','applicationId','ownerRef','projectRef','resource']);
+ check(s.accountId==='85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec'&&s.workerName==='agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary'&&s.namespaceId==='d6fc71552de7494a863aca20365a572d'&&s.applicationId===s.namespaceId&&initialHex(s.ownerRef)&&initialHex(s.projectRef)&&s.resource==='https://delegated-canary.agentfriendlyweb.dev/mcp');
+ const o=initialFields(v.original,['containerId','applicationId','programHash','sourceRevision']);
+ check(initialHex(o.containerId)&&o.applicationId===s.applicationId&&initialHex(o.programHash)&&initialHex(o.sourceRevision,40));
+ const p=initialFields(v.publication,['artifactKey','workerHash','activationKey']);
+ check(Object.values(p).every(x=>initialHex(x))&&p.workerHash===a.workerHash&&p.artifactKey===createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify([s.accountId,s.workerName,a.workerHash])).digest('hex'));
+ check(Array.isArray(v.cancellations)&&v.cancellations.length>=1&&v.cancellations.length<=8);
+ for(const c of v.cancellations){initialFields(c,['grantId','containerId','programHash','sourceRevision','image','closedAt','revokedAt','monitorSequence','monitorRevision','monitorRevokedAt']);check(id(c.grantId)&&initialHex(c.containerId)&&initialHex(c.programHash)&&initialHex(c.sourceRevision,40)&&c.image==='cloudflare/debian-trixie'&&time(c.closedAt)&&time(c.monitorRevokedAt)&&Number.isSafeInteger(c.monitorSequence)&&c.monitorSequence>0&&Number.isSafeInteger(c.monitorRevision)&&c.monitorRevision>0&&typeof c.revokedAt==='string'&&Number.isSafeInteger(Date.parse(c.revokedAt))&&new Date(Date.parse(c.revokedAt)).toISOString()===c.revokedAt);}
+ check(typeof v.retirementPolicy==='string'&&v.retirementPolicy.length>0&&Buffer.byteLength(v.retirementPolicy)<=4096&&uuid(v.withdrawalRequestId)&&uuid(v.saveIdempotencyKey)&&initialHex(v.trialId));
+ const l=initialFields(v.legacy,['control','controlHash','preparedDispatch','preparedDispatchHash','reductionDispatch','reductionDispatchHash','restoration','retirement','sourceRevision','sourceHash']);
+ check(['control','preparedDispatch','reductionDispatch'].every(k=>typeof l[k]==='string'&&l[k].length>0)&&['controlHash','preparedDispatchHash','reductionDispatchHash','sourceHash'].every(k=>initialHex(l[k]))&&initialHex(l.sourceRevision,40)&&l.restoration===null&&l.retirement===null);return v;
+}
+function selection(v){
+ initialFields(v,['subject','project','clientId','resource','goal','access','limiter']);initialFields(v.project,['projectId','revision','synthetic']);initialFields(v.access,['teamDomain','audience']);
+ check(id(v.subject)&&id(v.project.projectId)&&Number.isSafeInteger(v.project.revision)&&v.project.revision>0&&v.project.synthetic===true&&id(v.clientId)&&v.resource==='https://delegated-canary.agentfriendlyweb.dev/mcp'&&v.goal==='content'&&/^[a-z0-9-]+\.cloudflareaccess\.com$/.test(v.access.teamDomain)&&id(v.access.audience));
+ const l=initialFields(v.limiter,['name','type','namespace_id','simple']);initialFields(l.simple,['limit','period']);
+ check(l.name==='DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER'&&l.type==='ratelimit'&&typeof l.namespace_id==='string'&&/^\d+$/.test(l.namespace_id)&&Number.isSafeInteger(l.simple.limit)&&l.simple.limit>0&&[10,60].includes(l.simple.period));
+}
+function evidence(v,M,tree){
+ initialFields(v,['purpose','reader','artifactReceiptHash','metadataProofHash','bindingsHash','legacyHash','historyHash']);initialFields(v.reader,['sourceRevision','sourceTree']);
+ check(v.purpose==='afw.finite-historical-evidence.v1'&&v.reader.sourceRevision===M&&v.reader.sourceTree===tree&&['artifactReceiptHash','metadataProofHash','bindingsHash','legacyHash','historyHash'].every(k=>initialHex(v[k])));
+}
+const definitions={
+ InitialClaim:['afw.finite-initial-claim.v1',['intent','commandHash','ids','claimedAt','deadline','claimHash'],'claimHash',8192],
+ InitialMaterial:['afw.finite-initial-material.v1',['intent','claimHash','preconnectionId','qaBootstrap','historicalEvidence','ownerSelection','originalContext','observedAt','materialHash'],'materialHash',65536],
+ InitialSelector:['afw.finite-initial-selector.v1',['intentHash','claimHash','materialHash','sourceRevision','qaVersionId','preconnectionId'],null,2048],
+ InitialAck:['afw.finite-initial-material-ack.v1',['intentHash','claimHash','materialHash','selectorHash','acknowledgedAt'],null,2048]
+};
+function validate(kind,input,hashing=false){
+ check(Object.hasOwn(definitions,kind));const [purpose,keys,self,cap]=definitions[kind],v=initialOwn(input),names=['purpose',...keys];
+ if(hashing&&self&&!Object.hasOwn(v,self))names.splice(names.indexOf(self),1);
+ initialFields(v,names);check(v.purpose===purpose&&Buffer.byteLength(initialCanonical(v))<=cap);
+ for(const[k,x]of Object.entries(v)){if(k.endsWith('Hash'))check(initialHex(x));if(k==='sourceRevision')check(initialHex(x,40));if(k==='qaVersionId')check(uuid(x));if(k==='preconnectionId')check(initialHex(x));}
+ if(kind==='InitialClaim'){intent(v.intent);ids(v.ids);check(time(v.claimedAt)&&time(v.deadline)&&v.claimedAt>=v.intent.I0&&v.claimedAt<v.deadline&&v.deadline<=v.intent.initialDeadline&&v.deadline-v.claimedAt<=30000);}
+ if(kind==='InitialMaterial'){
+  const i=intent(v.intent);check(time(v.observedAt)&&v.observedAt>=i.I0&&v.observedAt<i.initialDeadline);selection(v.ownerSelection);
+  const q=v.qaBootstrap;initialFields(q,['purpose','record','recordHash','tagSha','primaryEvidence','currentPointer','observedAt','qaPublicationBridgeVerified','qaCodeProvenanceVerified','sourceIdentityVerified','scopeVerified','authorizesEffects','operationalReadiness','completePackage','repositoryWriterTrustAssumed','historicalRefNonmutationVerified']);
+  const r=decodeFiniteQaBootstrap(initialCanonical({record:q.record,recordHash:q.recordHash})).record;
+  check(q.purpose==='afw.finite-qa-bootstrap-evidence.v1'&&initialHex(q.tagSha,40)&&time(q.observedAt)&&q.qaPublicationBridgeVerified===true&&q.repositoryWriterTrustAssumed===true&&['qaCodeProvenanceVerified','sourceIdentityVerified','scopeVerified','authorizesEffects','operationalReadiness','completePackage','historicalRefNonmutationVerified'].every(k=>q[k]===false)&&r.sourceRevision===i.sourceRevision&&r.sourceTree===i.sourceTree&&r.qaLive.versionId===i.qaVersionId);
+  const pe=initialFields(q.primaryEvidence,['P0','Pend','jobId','jobStartedAt','stepStartedAt','stepCompletedAt','jobCompletedAt']),cp=initialFields(q.currentPointer,['versionId','deploymentId']);
+  check(['P0','Pend','jobId','jobStartedAt','stepStartedAt'].every(k=>pe[k]===r[k])&&time(pe.stepCompletedAt)&&time(pe.jobCompletedAt)&&pe.stepCompletedAt>=r.observedAt&&pe.jobCompletedAt>=pe.stepCompletedAt&&pe.jobCompletedAt<r.Pend&&pe.jobCompletedAt<=q.observedAt&&cp.versionId===r.qaLive.versionId&&cp.deploymentId===r.qaLive.deploymentId);
+  const p=validateFiniteInitialOriginalContext(v.originalContext);evidence(v.historicalEvidence,i.sourceRevision,i.sourceTree);
+  check(p.artifact.sourceRevision===i.sourceRevision&&p.artifact.sourceTree===i.sourceTree&&p.legacy.sourceRevision===i.sourceRevision&&p.legacy.sourceHash===initialHash('afw.finite-historical-evidence.v1',v.historicalEvidence));
+ }
+ if(kind==='InitialAck')check(time(v.acknowledgedAt));
+ if(self&&!hashing)check(v[self]===hashBody(kind,v));return v;
+}
+function hashBody(kind,v){const [domain,,self]=definitions[kind];return initialHash(domain,Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k])=>k!==self)));}
+export function hashFiniteInitialMaterialRecord(kind,value){return hashBody(kind,validate(kind,value,true));}
+export function validateFiniteInitialMaterialRecord(kind,value){return validate(kind,value);}
+export function encodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord(kind,value){return initialCanonical(validate(kind,value));}
+export function decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord(kind,text){check(typeof text==='string'&&Buffer.byteLength(text)<=65536);const v=validate(kind,JSON.parse(text));check(initialCanonical(v)===text);return v;}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-store.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-store.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-store.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-store.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,69 @@
+import {randomBytes,randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
+import {initialHash,initialFields,encodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as encode,decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as decode,hashFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as hash} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise as track} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+const PREFIX='afw.finite-initial-preparation.v1:',HEAD='afw.qa-finite-trial.v1:head';
+// Isolate-local denial only, including a transaction that failed before durable
+// evidence existed. Restart continuity requires the later native owning entry;
+// this WeakSet is not a durable claim or an admission.
+const attempted=new WeakSet();
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial storage unavailable');};
+const check=l=>{initialFields(l,['signal','assertCurrent']);if(!(l.signal instanceof AbortSignal)||l.signal.aborted)fail();l.assertCurrent();};
+const seal=(kind,value,key)=>decode(kind,encode(kind,{...value,[key]:hash(kind,value)}));
+/** Private factual append-only storage, never permission/admission. */
+export function createFiniteInitialMaterialStore({storage}){
+ const witnesses=new WeakMap(),transaction=storage?.transaction,get=storage?.get;if(typeof transaction!=='function'||typeof get!=='function')fail();
+ const current=l=>{check(l);if(storage.transaction!==transaction||storage.get!==get)fail();};
+ const read=async(key,kind,l)=>{current(l);const raw=await track(Reflect.apply(get,storage,[key]));current(l);return raw===undefined?null:decode(kind,raw);};
+ const tx=async(l,fn)=>{current(l);const result=await track(Reflect.apply(transaction,storage,[async t=>{
+  current(l);const tg=t.get,tp=t.put;if(typeof tg!=='function'||typeof tp!=='function')fail();
+  const guard=()=>{current(l);if(t.get!==tg||t.put!==tp)fail();};
+  const io={async get(k){guard();const v=await Reflect.apply(tg,t,[k]);guard();return v;},async put(k,kind,v){guard();await Reflect.apply(tp,t,[k,encode(kind,v)]);guard();}};
+  const out=await fn(io);guard();return out;
+ }]));current(l);return result;};
+ return Object.freeze({
+  async available(l){current(l);if(attempted.has(storage))return false;const result=await tx(l,async io=>{
+   for(const key of [PREFIX+'once',PREFIX+'selector',PREFIX+'ack',HEAD])if(await io.get(key)!==undefined)return false;return true;
+  });current(l);return result;},
+  async claim(intent,commandHash,deadline,l){
+   current(l);if(attempted.has(storage))fail();attempted.add(storage);
+   let allocated=null;
+   const record=await tx(l,async io=>{
+    for(const key of [PREFIX+'once',PREFIX+'selector',PREFIX+'ack',HEAD])if(await io.get(key)!==undefined)fail();
+    // Memoize allocation within this original call, including platform callback
+    // re-entry. A caller retry never reaches this point after a durable claim.
+    allocated??={preconnectionId:randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),trialId:randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),activationKey:randomBytes(32).toString('hex'),withdrawalRequestId:randomUUID(),saveIdempotencyKey:randomUUID()};
+    const v=seal('InitialClaim',{purpose:'afw.finite-initial-claim.v1',intent,commandHash,ids:allocated,claimedAt:Date.now(),deadline},'claimHash');
+    await io.put(PREFIX+'once','InitialClaim',v);return v;
+   });
+   const observed=await read(PREFIX+'once','InitialClaim',l);
+   if(encode('InitialClaim',observed)!==encode('InitialClaim',record))fail();return record;
+  },
+  async publish(claim,material,l){
+   const m=decode('InitialMaterial',encode('InitialMaterial',material)),intentHash=initialHash('afw.finite-initial-intent.v1',claim.intent);
+   if(m.claimHash!==claim.claimHash||m.preconnectionId!==claim.ids.preconnectionId||initialHash('intent',m.intent)!==initialHash('intent',claim.intent))fail();
+   const o=m.originalContext;if(o.trialId!==claim.ids.trialId||o.publication.activationKey!==claim.ids.activationKey||o.withdrawalRequestId!==claim.ids.withdrawalRequestId||o.saveIdempotencyKey!==claim.ids.saveIdempotencyKey)fail();
+   const selector={purpose:'afw.finite-initial-selector.v1',intentHash,claimHash:claim.claimHash,materialHash:m.materialHash,sourceRevision:claim.intent.sourceRevision,qaVersionId:claim.intent.qaVersionId,preconnectionId:claim.ids.preconnectionId};
+   const key=PREFIX+'material:'+m.materialHash;
+   await tx(l,async io=>{
+    if(await io.get(HEAD)!==undefined||await io.get(PREFIX+'once')!==encode('InitialClaim',claim))fail();
+    for(const k of [key,PREFIX+'selector',PREFIX+'ack'])if(await io.get(k)!==undefined)fail();
+    await io.put(key,'InitialMaterial',m);await io.put(PREFIX+'selector','InitialSelector',selector);
+   });
+   // Only THIS actual transaction ACK can create this factual witness.
+   current(l);const acknowledgedAt=Date.now();if(acknowledgedAt>=claim.deadline)fail();
+   const ack={purpose:'afw.finite-initial-material-ack.v1',intentHash,claimHash:claim.claimHash,materialHash:m.materialHash,selectorHash:hash('InitialSelector',selector),acknowledgedAt};
+   await tx(l,async io=>{
+    if(await io.get(HEAD)!==undefined||await io.get(PREFIX+'once')!==encode('InitialClaim',claim)||await io.get(key)!==encode('InitialMaterial',m)||await io.get(PREFIX+'selector')!==encode('InitialSelector',selector)||await io.get(PREFIX+'ack')!==undefined)fail();
+    await io.put(PREFIX+'ack','InitialAck',ack);
+   });
+   for(const[k,kind,v]of [[PREFIX+'once','InitialClaim',claim],[key,'InitialMaterial',m],[PREFIX+'selector','InitialSelector',selector],[PREFIX+'ack','InitialAck',ack]]){
+    if(encode(kind,await read(k,kind,l))!==encode(kind,v))fail();
+   }
+   current(l);witnesses.set(m,{claim:encode('InitialClaim',claim),selector:encode('InitialSelector',selector),ack:encode('InitialAck',ack)});return m;
+  },
+  assertPublished(material,claim,selector,ack,l){
+   current(l);const w=witnesses.get(material);
+   if(!w||w.claim!==encode('InitialClaim',claim)||w.selector!==encode('InitialSelector',selector)||w.ack!==encode('InitialAck',ack))fail();
+  }
+ });
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-material.mjs

+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {readPrivateQaFiniteCandidate} from './mcp-qa-finite-candidate-residency.mjs';
+import {readPrivateQaFiniteBootstrap} from './mcp-qa-finite-bootstrap-native.mjs';
~~~~~~~~~~
