~~~~~~~~~~diff
+import {readPrivateQaFiniteHistoricalSource} from './mcp-qa-finite-historical-source.mjs';
+import {createDelegatedProjectRepository} from './delegated-project-repository.mjs';
+import {createDelegatedOAuthStore} from './delegated-oauth-store.mjs';
+import {resolveSuccessorPreparation} from './mcp-successor-manifest.mjs';
+import {createFiniteInitialMaterialStore} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-store.mjs';
+import {initialFields as fields,initialOwn as own,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex,initialInstanceId,validateFiniteInitialOriginalContext,encodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as encode,decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as decode,hashFiniteInitialMaterialRecord as recordHash} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {observeFiniteOwnerStageWork,retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise as track} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+const GH='https://api.github.com/repos/tokenizartinfo-ops/agent-friendly-web',CEILING=Date.parse('2026-11-03T02:59:00Z');
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial material unavailable');},pending=()=>Object.freeze({status:'admin_pending',mayRetry:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+const sha=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
+/** UNMOUNTED factual constructor. No effects, authority registration or RPC. */
+export function createPrivateQaFiniteInitialMaterial(options){
+ fields(options,['ctx','env']);const {ctx,env}=options,storage=ctx?.storage,actor=ctx?.id,toString=actor?.toString,db=env?.RELEASE_DB,withSession=db?.withSession,prepare=db?.prepare,self=env?.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,selfName=self?.idFromName,node=env?.AFW_RELEASE_EVENTS_NODE,nodeName=node?.idFromName,nodeString=node?.idFromString,metadata=env?.AFW_QA_VERSION,qaVersionId=metadata?.id,credential=env?.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN,fetcher=globalThis.fetch,transaction=storage?.transaction,get=storage?.get;
+ if([toString,withSession,prepare,selfName,nodeName,nodeString,fetcher,transaction,get].some(x=>typeof x!=='function'))fail();
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+export async function readFiniteInitialOldGuard(options,originalLease){

+  if(!active||signal.aborted||controller.signal.aborted||Date.now()<entered||Date.now()-entered>=5000||performance.now()<mono||performance.now()-mono>=5000||options.env!==env||env.AFW_MCP_CLOSURE_READ_TOKEN!==token||globalThis.fetch!==fetcher||originalLease.signal!==signal||originalLease.assertCurrent!==assertCurrent)fail();
+  assertCurrent();
+ };
+ const stopped=new Promise((_,reject)=>{onAbort=()=>{active=false;controller.abort();reject(Error('Finite initial old guard unavailable'));};signal.addEventListener('abort',onAbort,{once:true});timer=setTimeout(onAbort,5000);});

+  check();
+  const actual=retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise(Promise.resolve().then(async()=>{
+   check();let reader,response;
+   try{
+    response=await fetcher(URL,{method:'GET',redirect:'manual',cache:'no-store',credentials:'omit',referrerPolicy:'no-referrer',signal:controller.signal,headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+token,Accept:'application/json','Cache-Control':'no-cache'}});check();
+    const date=Date.parse(response?.headers?.get('date')??''),length=response?.headers?.get('content-length');
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-records.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-records.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-records.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-records.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {initialUploadSha as sha} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs';
+import {QA_CLOUD_CLOSED_PINS} from './mcp-qa-cloud-closed-pins.mjs';
+import {restorationConfigurationHash} from './mcp-restoration-configuration.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial upload record unavailable');};
+const UUID=/^[a-f0-9]{8}(-[a-f0-9]{4}){3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/;
+const common=['entryHash','materialHash','sourceRevision','sourceTree','qaVersionId','initialInstanceId'];
+const schemas={
+ Backup:[...common,'baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId','moduleHash','moduleBytes','configurationHash','rawConfigurationHash','historicalSourceRevision','historicalSourceTree','rawConfiguration','configuration','chunks','retainedIds'],
+ Chunk:['manifestHash','index','bytes','sha256','content'],
+ Send:[...common,'manifestHash','moduleHash','baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId','effect','accountId','workerName','method','path','bodyHash','requestHash','issuedAt','deadline'],
+ Ack:['sendHash','uploadedVersionId','acknowledgedAt'],
+ Receipt:[...common,'manifestHash','sendHash','ackHash','uploadedVersionId','moduleHash','configurationHash','baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId','beforeObservedAt','afterObservedAt','recordedAt','authorizesEffects','completePackage','operationalReadiness']
+};
+const purpose=k=>'afw.finite-initial-upload-'+k.toLowerCase()+'.v1';
+function validate(kind,value){
+ if(!Object.hasOwn(schemas,kind))fail();
+ const v=own(fields(value,['purpose',...schemas[kind],'recordHash']));
+ if(v.purpose!==purpose(kind))fail();
+ const {recordHash,...body}=v;if(!hex(recordHash)||hash(v.purpose,body)!==recordHash)fail();
+ for(const [k,x]of Object.entries(v)){
+  if(k.endsWith('Hash')&&!hex(x)||['sourceRevision','sourceTree','historicalSourceRevision','historicalSourceTree'].includes(k)&&!hex(x,40)||['qaVersionId','baselineVersionId','baselineDeploymentId','uploadedVersionId'].includes(k)&&!UUID.test(x))fail();
+ }
+ if(common.every(k=>Object.hasOwn(v,k))&&(!/^afw-finite-initial-[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(v.initialInstanceId)))fail();
+ if(Object.hasOwn(v,'baselineVersionId')&&v.baselineVersionId!=='2607abae-cec3-4e35-89eb-8b8572262b54')fail();
+ for(const k of ['issuedAt','deadline','acknowledgedAt','beforeObservedAt','afterObservedAt','recordedAt'])if(Object.hasOwn(v,k)&&(!Number.isSafeInteger(v[k])||v[k]<0))fail();
+ if(kind==='Chunk'){
+  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(v.index)||v.index<0||v.index>=64||!Number.isSafeInteger(v.bytes)||v.bytes<1||v.bytes>32768||!hex(v.sha256)||typeof v.content!=='string'||v.content.length!==4*Math.ceil(v.bytes/3))fail();
+  const bytes=Buffer.from(v.content,'base64');if(bytes.length!==v.bytes||bytes.toString('base64')!==v.content||sha(bytes)!==v.sha256)fail();
+ }
+ if(kind==='Backup'){
+  const p=QA_CLOUD_CLOSED_PINS.canary;if(v.moduleHash!==p.module.sha256||v.moduleBytes!==p.module.bytes||v.baselineDeploymentId!==p.deploymentId||v.configurationHash!==p.configurationHash||restorationConfigurationHash(v.configuration)!==v.configurationHash||restorationConfigurationHash(v.rawConfiguration)!==v.rawConfigurationHash)fail();
+  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(v.moduleBytes)||v.moduleBytes<1||v.moduleBytes>2097152||!Array.isArray(v.chunks)||v.chunks.length!==Math.ceil(v.moduleBytes/32768)||v.chunks.length>64)fail();
+  let size=0;for(const [index,c]of v.chunks.entries()){fields(c,['index','bytes','sha256']);if(c.index!==index||!hex(c.sha256)||c.bytes!==Math.min(32768,v.moduleBytes-size))fail();size+=c.bytes;}
+  fields(v.retainedIds,['preconnectionId','trialId','activationKey','withdrawalRequestId','saveIdempotencyKey']);
+  for(const k of ['preconnectionId','trialId','activationKey'])if(!hex(v.retainedIds[k]))fail();
+  for(const k of ['withdrawalRequestId','saveIdempotencyKey'])if(!UUID.test(v.retainedIds[k]))fail();
+  for(const cfg of [v.rawConfiguration,v.configuration]){
+   if(!Array.isArray(cfg.bindings))fail();
+   for(const b of cfg.bindings)if(b.type==='secret_text')fields(b,['name','type']);
+  }
+ }
+ if(kind==='Send'&&(v.effect!=='upload'||v.accountId!=='85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec'||v.workerName!=='agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary'||v.method!=='POST'||v.path!=='/accounts/85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec/workers/workers/agent-friendly-web-delegated-canary/versions?deploy=false'||v.deadline<=v.issuedAt||v.deadline-v.issuedAt>5000))fail();
+ if(kind==='Receipt'&&(v.authorizesEffects!==false||v.completePackage!==false||v.operationalReadiness!==false||v.afterObservedAt<v.beforeObservedAt||v.recordedAt<v.afterObservedAt))fail();
+ return v;
+}
+/** Pure bounded factual codecs only: no persistence, caller admission or capability. */
+export function sealFiniteInitialUploadRecord(kind,value){
+ const body=own({purpose:purpose(kind),...own(value)});return validate(kind,{...body,recordHash:hash(body.purpose,body)});
+}
+export function encodeFiniteInitialUploadRecord(kind,value){return canonical(validate(kind,value));}
+export function decodeFiniteInitialUploadRecord(kind,raw){
+ if(typeof raw!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(raw)>65536)fail();
+ const v=validate(kind,JSON.parse(raw));if(canonical(v)!==raw)fail();return v;
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-initial-upload-wire.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialCanonical as canonical,decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {finitePublicProfileTemplate} from './mcp-qa-finite-profile.mjs';
+import {qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile} from './mcp-qa-finite-connection-profile.mjs';
+import {restorationConfigurationHash} from './mcp-restoration-configuration.mjs';
+import {qualifyFiniteWorkerVersion} from './mcp-finite-primary-inventory.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite initial upload wire unavailable');};
+export const initialUploadSha=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
+const sha=initialUploadSha,BASE='2607abae-cec3-4e35-89eb-8b8572262b54';
+const CONFIG=['compatibility_date','compatibility_flags','main_module','exports','bindings','usage_model','containers'];
+const READONLY=['id','number','startup_time_ms','annotations','urls','source','created_on','author_id','author_email','modules'];
+const same=(a,b)=>canonical(a)===canonical(b);
+/** Pure supported profile projection; neither caller data nor this output grants upload. */
+export function buildFiniteInitialUpload(material,baseline,bytes){
+ const m=decodeFiniteInitialMaterialRecord('InitialMaterial',canonical(material)),p=m.ownerSelection;
+ if(!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)||bytes.length<1||bytes.length>2097152||sha(bytes)!==m.originalContext.artifact.workerHash)fail();
+ const cfg=structuredClone(finitePublicProfileTemplate('canaryLive'));
+ cfg.bindings=cfg.bindings.map(b=>b.name.endsWith('_ENABLED')?{name:b.name,type:'plain_text',text:'false'}:b.privateSlot?{name:b.name,type:'plain_text',text:b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_CLIENT_ID'?p.clientId:b.name==='AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_PROJECT_ID'?p.project.projectId:''}:b);
+ cfg.bindings.push({name:'ACCESS_TEAM_DOMAIN',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.teamDomain},{name:'ACCESS_AUD',type:'plain_text',text:p.access.audience},{name:'AFW_OAUTH_PILOT_EXPIRES_AT',type:'plain_text',text:new Date(m.intent.I0+900000).toISOString()},structuredClone(p.limiter),{name:'AFW_DOSSIER_MONITORING_OWNER_POLICY',type:'plain_text',text:''});
+ qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile({phase:'closed_ui',metadata:cfg});
+ // Every physical/secret binding must already exist under the same actual ID;
+ // the two explicitly reviewed profile references are the only exceptions.
+ const physical=b=>!['plain_text','version_metadata'].includes(b.type);
+ const original=baseline.configuration.bindings.filter(physical),next=cfg.bindings.filter(physical).filter(b=>b.name!=='AFW_FINITE_TRIAL_CONTROL');
+ if(!same(original.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)),next.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))))fail();
+ const configuration=own(cfg),body=JSON.stringify({...configuration,containers:[{class_name:'AfwOwnEventNode'}],bindings:configuration.bindings.map(b=>b.type==='secret_text'?{name:b.name,type:'inherit',version_id:BASE}:b),modules:[{name:'index.mjs',content_type:'application/javascript+module',content_base64:Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64')}]});
+ return {configuration,body,bodyHash:sha(body)};
+}
+/** Strict provider normalization; reject dangerous/unknown fields before copying. */
+export function qualifyFiniteInitialUploadedVersion(v,versionId,configuration,bytes){
+ fields(v,[...CONFIG,...READONLY.filter(k=>Object.hasOwn(v,k)),...Object.hasOwn(v,'cache_options')?['cache_options']:[]]);
+ if(v.id!==versionId||!Array.isArray(v.urls)||v.urls.length)fail();
+ // Raw module is large; every other field is copied under the 64KiB bound.
+ const raw=own(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k])=>k!=='modules')));
+ const cfg=structuredClone(Object.fromEntries(CONFIG.map(k=>[k,raw[k]])));
+ fields(cfg.exports,['default','AfwNodeControl','AfwOwnEventNode']);
+ for(const [name,e]of Object.entries(cfg.exports)){
+  const keys=name==='AfwOwnEventNode'?['type','storage','container']:['type'];
+  fields(e,[...keys,...Object.hasOwn(e,'state')?['state']:[],...Object.hasOwn(e,'cache')?['cache']:[]]);
+  if(Object.hasOwn(e,'state')){if(e.state!=='created')fail();delete e.state;}
+  if(Object.hasOwn(e,'cache')){fields(e.cache,['enabled']);if(name==='AfwOwnEventNode'||e.cache.enabled!==false)fail();delete e.cache;}
+ }
+ if(Object.hasOwn(raw,'cache_options')){const keys=['enabled','cross_version_cache'].filter(k=>Object.hasOwn(raw.cache_options??{},k));if(!keys.length)fail();fields(raw.cache_options,keys);if(keys.some(k=>raw.cache_options[k]!==false))fail();}
+ if(!same(cfg,configuration))fail();
+ qualifyFiniteConnectionProfile({phase:'closed_ui',metadata:cfg});
+ fields(v.modules?.[0],['name','content_type','content_base64']);
+ qualifyFiniteWorkerVersion({metadata:{...cfg,id:v.id,urls:v.urls,modules:v.modules},pin:{versionId,module:{name:'index.mjs',sha256:sha(bytes),bytes:bytes.length},configurationHash:restorationConfigurationHash(cfg)},role:'canary',closed:true});
+ return own(cfg);
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-inventory.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-inventory.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-inventory.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-inventory.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin,readPrivateQaFiniteControlSnapshotUnderCoordinator} from './mcp-qa-finite-trial-control.mjs';
+import {readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchMaterial} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-source.mjs';
+import {readFiniteAfwWorkflowInventory,consumePrivateFiniteLaunchInventory,AFW_FIXED_WORKFLOWS} from './mcp-fixed-terminal-workflows.mjs';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data,finiteSame as same,finiteTime as time} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite launch inventory unavailable');};
+const contexts=new WeakMap(),coordinators=new WeakMap(),observations=new WeakMap();
+/** Fixed parser lookup only. No caller can mint this invocation token. */
+export function readPrivateFiniteLaunchObservation(token){
+ const record=observations.get(token);if(!record||record.consumed)fail();record.check();record.consumed=true;return record.value;
+}
+const unavailable=()=>Object.freeze({kind:'unavailable',authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+/** INTERNAL operation entry only: create BEFORE source hydration. All later
+ * helpers retain this exact absolute parent and signal; no fresh five seconds. */
+export function createPrivateQaFiniteLaunchParent({now=Date.now,deadline}={}){
+ if(typeof now!=='function')fail();const entered=now(),mono=performance.now(),controller=new AbortController();
+ if(!time(entered)||!time(deadline))fail();const end=Math.min(deadline,entered+5000);let last=entered,lastMono=mono;
+ const check=()=>{
+  const at=now(),m=performance.now();
+  if(controller.signal.aborted||!time(at)||at<last||!Number.isFinite(m)||m<lastMono||at>=end||m-mono>=end-entered){controller.abort();fail();}
+  last=at;lastMono=m;return at;
+ };check();return Object.freeze({check,signal:controller.signal,deadline:end});
+}
+function budget(parent){
+ const p=fields(parent,['check','signal','deadline']);
+ if(typeof p.check!=='function'||!(p.signal instanceof AbortSignal)||!time(p.deadline))fail();
+ const check=()=>{if(['check','signal','deadline'].some(k=>parent[k]!==p[k])||p.signal.aborted)fail();const at=p.check();if(!time(at)||at>=p.deadline)fail();return at;};
+ if(p.deadline-check()>5000)fail();return check;
+}
+async function bounded(parent,operation){
+ const checkParent=budget(parent);let active=true,timer,abort;
+ const check=()=>{if(!active)fail();return checkParent();};
+ try{
+  const result=await Promise.race([
+   Promise.resolve().then(()=>{check();return operation(check);}),
+   new Promise((_,reject)=>{
+    const stop=()=>{active=false;reject(Error('Finite launch inventory unavailable'));};
+    abort=stop;parent.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});
+    timer=setTimeout(stop,parent.deadline-check());
+   })
+  ]);check();return result;
+ }finally{active=false;clearTimeout(timer);if(abort)parent.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abort);}
+}
+async function retain({ctx,env,context,parent}){
+ const check=budget(parent);check();
+ const value=await bounded(parent,()=>readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchMaterial({ctx,env,context,parent}));check();
+ const m=fields(value,['ctx','env','plan','request','execution']);
+ if(m.ctx!==ctx||m.env!==env||typeof m.plan!=='string'||typeof m.request!=='function')fail();
+ const existing=contexts.get(context);
+ if(existing){if(existing.ctx!==ctx||existing.env!==env||existing.parent!==parent||['plan','request','execution'].some(k=>existing.material[k]!==m[k]))fail();return existing;}
+ const nativePin=createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin({ctx,env,sourceRevision:m.execution.sourceRevision});check();
+ const kept={ctx,env,storage:ctx.storage,parent,material:m,execution:data(m.execution),nativePin,witnessedUnspent:false};contexts.set(context,kept);return kept;
+}
+/** INTERNAL shared actor coordinator. Task2 must route EVERY SEND transition
+ * through this function and use check before/after every await. No RPC mount.
+ * Queue timeout cannot release a predecessor's slot or grant a new budget.
+ * Caller expiry fences authority but does not cancel issued work: the slot
+ * remains occupied until the actual callback settles, even after caller failure. */
+export async function withPrivateQaFiniteLaunchCoordinator(options,operation){
+ const o=fields(options,['ctx','env','context','parent']),{ctx,env,context,parent}=o;
+ if(typeof operation!=='function')fail();
+ const kept=await retain({ctx,env,context,parent});budget(parent)();
+ const previous=coordinators.get(ctx)??Promise.resolve();let release,work;
+ const gate=new Promise(resolve=>{release=resolve;});
+ const tail=previous.catch(()=>{}).then(()=>gate);coordinators.set(ctx,tail);
+ const releaseSlot=()=>{release();if(coordinators.get(ctx)===tail)void tail.then(()=>{if(coordinators.get(ctx)===tail)coordinators.delete(ctx);});};
+ try{
+  await bounded(parent,()=>previous);
+  return await bounded(parent,check=>{
+   work=(async()=>{
+    if(kept.storage!==ctx.storage||!same(kept.execution,kept.material.execution))fail();check();
+    return operation(check);
+   })();
+   return work;
+  });
+ }finally{
+  // Observe both outcomes without delaying the bounded caller or releasing on
+  // its timeout. A callback that never settles deliberately keeps the actor held.
+  if(work)void work.then(releaseSlot,releaseSlot);else releaseSlot();
+ }
+}
+/** Source-only construction; caller shapes never resolve the private C2 token.
+ * Full enumeration and the synchronous consumer are fixed imports, not callbacks.
+ * The returned result is facts only; it cannot be replayed into a public validator. */
+export async function createPrivateQaFiniteLaunchInventory(options){
+ let kept;
+ try{
+  const o=fields(options,['ctx','env','context','parent']);kept=await retain(o);
+ }catch{return Object.freeze({observeBeforeCreation:async()=>unavailable()});}
+ const {ctx,env,parent,material:m,nativePin}=kept,context=options.context,storage=ctx.storage;
+ return Object.freeze({async observeBeforeCreation(...args){
+  if(args.length)return unavailable();
+  try{return await withPrivateQaFiniteLaunchCoordinator({ctx,env,context,parent},async check=>{
+   const snapshot=async()=>{check();if(ctx.storage!==storage)fail();const s=await readPrivateQaFiniteControlSnapshotUnderCoordinator({storage,nativePin,plan:m.plan,parent});check();return s;};
+   const before=await snapshot();
+   // Pending/armed without a prepared receipt is accepted only after this SAME
+   // invocation actually observed the unspent head. Reconstruction cannot adopt it.
+   if(before.state&&!before.prepared&&!before.send&&!kept.witnessedUnspent)fail();
+   const read=async(path,query)=>{
+    check();const allowed=path==='/workflows'||AFW_FIXED_WORKFLOWS.some(s=>path==='/workflows/'+s.name||path==='/workflows/'+s.name+'/instances');
+    if(!allowed)fail();
+    const response=await m.request({method:'GET',path,query,signal:parent.signal},check);check();
+    if(response?.status!==200||response.success!==true||!Array.isArray(response.errors)||response.errors.length||!Object.hasOwn(response,'result'))fail();
+    return {value:response.result,info:response.result_info};
+   };
+   const inventory=await readFiniteAfwWorkflowInventory({read,assertCurrent:check});check();
+   const after=await snapshot();
+   if(!same(before,after))fail();
+   // Deliberately no await between this consumption and releasing the coordinator.
+   if(!same(kept.execution,m.execution))fail();
+   const token=Object.freeze({});
+   observations.set(token,{value:{inventory,snapshot:after,execution:kept.execution,observedAt:check()},check,consumed:false});
+   try{
+    const observation=consumePrivateFiniteLaunchInventory(token);
+    if(!after.state)kept.witnessedUnspent=true;
+    return data({...observation,authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+   }finally{observations.delete(token);}
+  });}catch{return unavailable();}
+ }});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite launch record unavailable');};
+const hex=(v,n=64)=>typeof v==='string'&&new RegExp('^[a-f0-9]{'+n+'}$').test(v);
+const time=v=>Number.isSafeInteger(v)&&v>=0&&v<=8640000000000000;
+const id=v=>typeof v==='string'&&/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/.test(v);
+function fields(v,keys){
+ if(!v||Object.getPrototypeOf(v)!==Object.prototype||Reflect.ownKeys(v).length!==keys.length)fail();
+ const out={};for(const k of keys){const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v,k);if(!d?.enumerable||!Object.hasOwn(d,'value'))fail();out[k]=d.value;}return Object.freeze(out);
+}
+const canonical=v=>JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(v).sort().map(k=>[k,v[k]])));
+const digest=(domain,v)=>createHash('sha256').update(domain+'\n'+canonical(v)).digest('hex');
+export function finiteLauncherInstanceId(value){
+ const v=fields(value,['intentId','sourceRevision']);if(!id(v.intentId)||!hex(v.sourceRevision,40))fail();
+ return 'afw-finite-launch-'+digest('afw.finite-launcher.v1',v);
+}
+export function validateFiniteLaunchPolicy(value){
+ const {T0,launchPolicy}=fields(value,['T0','launchPolicy']),p=fields(launchPolicy,['armAckBy','launchAt','lastCreateAt']);
+ if(!time(T0)||!time(p.armAckBy)||!time(p.launchAt)||!time(p.lastCreateAt)||p.armAckBy!==T0+90000||p.launchAt!==T0+150000||p.lastCreateAt!==T0+160000)fail();return p;
+}
+const IDENTITY=['intentId','finitePlanHash','controlHash','sourceRevision','qaVersionId','actorId','launcherId','nominalId','bundleHash','nominalClaimHash'];
+function identity(v){
+ if(!id(v.intentId)||!hex(v.sourceRevision,40)||typeof v.qaVersionId!=='string'||!(/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/).test(v.qaVersionId)||['finitePlanHash','controlHash','actorId','bundleHash','nominalClaimHash'].some(k=>!hex(v[k]))||v.launcherId!==finiteLauncherInstanceId({intentId:v.intentId,sourceRevision:v.sourceRevision})||v.nominalId!=='afw-release-'+v.bundleHash||v.nominalId===v.launcherId)fail();
+}
+export function validateFiniteLaunchPrepared(value){
+ const v=fields(value,['purpose',...IDENTITY,'guardStateHash','preparedAt','armAckBy','launchAt','lastCreateAt']);identity(v);
+ const T0=v.armAckBy-90000;validateFiniteLaunchPolicy({T0,launchPolicy:{armAckBy:v.armAckBy,launchAt:v.launchAt,lastCreateAt:v.lastCreateAt}});
+ if(v.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-launch-prepared.v1'||!hex(v.guardStateHash)||!time(v.preparedAt)||v.preparedAt<T0||v.preparedAt>v.armAckBy)fail();return v;
+}
+export function validateFiniteChildCreateSend(value){
+ const v=fields(value,['purpose',...IDENTITY,'preparedHash','bindingHash','issuedAt','deadline']);identity(v);
+ if(v.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-child-create-send.v1'||!hex(v.preparedHash)||!hex(v.bindingHash)||!time(v.issuedAt)||!time(v.deadline)||v.deadline<160000||v.issuedAt<v.deadline-10000||v.issuedAt>=v.deadline)fail();return v;
+}
+export function validateFiniteChildCreateAck(value){
+ const v=fields(value,['purpose','intentId','controlHash','sendHash','nominalId','acknowledgedAt']);
+ if(v.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-child-create-ack.v1'||!id(v.intentId)||!hex(v.controlHash)||!hex(v.sendHash)||typeof v.nominalId!=='string'||!/^afw-release-[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(v.nominalId)||!time(v.acknowledgedAt))fail();return v;
+}
+export function hashFiniteLaunchRecord(value){
+ const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value??{},'purpose');if(!d||!Object.hasOwn(d,'value'))fail();
+ const validators={'afw.qa-finite-launch-prepared.v1':validateFiniteLaunchPrepared,'afw.qa-finite-child-create-send.v1':validateFiniteChildCreateSend,'afw.qa-finite-child-create-ack.v1':validateFiniteChildCreateAck};if(typeof d.value!=='string'||!Object.hasOwn(validators,d.value))fail();const v=validators[d.value](value);return digest(v.purpose,v);
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch-source.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+/** Fixed C2 source boundary. No runtime registration, env material or caller DTO.
+ * C2 must qualify actual private setup completion, full source/binding coverage,
+ * exact native launcher/observer provenance and fixed GET custody before returning
+ * an opaque context. Task1 deliberately cannot install those missing facts. */
+const unavailable=()=>{throw Error('Finite launch source unavailable');};
+export const resolvePrivateQaFiniteLaunchContext=unavailable;
+export const readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchMaterial=unavailable;
+
+/** Fixed private source-produced operations; exact {controlPrimary,guardActions}.
+ * All operations must retain the original parent and await underlying work.
+ * C2 must qualify current setup, successful launcher prepare step and READY;
+ * this seam supplies no registration, env DTO or caller-controlled capability. */
+export const readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchOperations=unavailable;
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launch.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,148 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {resolvePrivateQaFiniteLaunchContext,readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchMaterial,readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchOperations} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-source.mjs';
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteLaunchInventory,withPrivateQaFiniteLaunchCoordinator} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-inventory.mjs';
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteTrialControl,createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin,readPrivateQaFiniteControlSnapshotUnderCoordinator} from './mcp-qa-finite-trial-control.mjs';
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteTrialGuard} from './mcp-qa-finite-trial-guard.mjs';
+import {validateFiniteLaunchPolicy,validateFiniteLaunchPrepared,validateFiniteChildCreateSend,validateFiniteChildCreateAck,hashFiniteLaunchRecord} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data,finiteSame as same,finiteHash as hash,finiteHex as hex} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+const ROOT='afw.qa-finite-trial.v1',JOURNAL='afw.qa-finite-child-create.v1:',GUARD='afw.qa-finite-guard.v1:';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite launch unavailable');};
+const result=(status,extra={})=>data({status,...extra,mayRetry:false,authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+const digest=(domain,value)=>createHash('sha256').update(domain+'\n'+JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');
+const sorted=rows=>[...rows].sort(([a],[b])=>a<b?-1:a>b?1:0);
+function method(object,key){
+ for(let p=object,n=0;p&&n<32;p=Object.getPrototypeOf(p),n++){
+  const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(p,key);if(d){if(!Object.hasOwn(d,'value')||typeof d.value!=='function')fail();return d.value;}
+ }fail();
+}
+function own(object,key){const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(object??{},key);if(!d?.enumerable||!Object.hasOwn(d,'value'))fail();return d.value;}
+/** Fixed native entry only. The ONE parent is created before hydration by the DO.
+ * All phase narrowing retains that exact parent/context/start. No caller-supplied
+ * source, binding, prepared receipt or successful DTO is accepted. */
+export async function runPrivateQaFiniteLaunchOperation(options){
+ let active=true,timer,abort,stop,phaseEnd=Infinity;
+ try{
+  const {ctx,env,operation,refs,parent}=fields(options,['ctx','env','operation','refs','parent']);
+  if(!['prepare','create','read'].includes(operation))fail();
+  const keys=operation==='prepare'?['intentId','sourceRevision']:['intentId','controlHash'],r=fields(refs,keys);
+  if(typeof r.intentId!=='string'||!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/.test(r.intentId)||!hex(r[keys[1]],operation==='prepare'?40:64))fail();
+  const b=fields(parent,['check','signal','deadline']);
+  if(typeof b.check!=='function'||!(b.signal instanceof AbortSignal))fail();
+  const storage=ctx?.storage,transaction=method(storage,'transaction'),get=method(storage,'get');
+  const binding=own(env,'AFW_QA_NAMED_CONFIRMATION'),create=method(binding,'create');
+  const check=()=>{
+   if(!active||b.signal.aborted||ctx.storage!==storage||method(storage,'transaction')!==transaction||method(storage,'get')!==get||own(env,'AFW_QA_NAMED_CONFIRMATION')!==binding||method(binding,'create')!==create||['check','signal','deadline'].some(k=>parent[k]!==b[k]))fail();
+   const at=b.check();if(at>=Math.min(b.deadline,phaseEnd))fail();return at;
+  };
+  const timeout=new Promise((_,reject)=>{stop=()=>{active=false;reject(Error('Finite launch unavailable'));};});
+  const narrow=end=>{phaseEnd=Math.min(phaseEnd,end);clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(stop,Math.min(b.deadline,phaseEnd)-check());};
+  abort=stop;b.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});narrow(b.deadline);
+  const work=async()=>{
+   check();const context=await resolvePrivateQaFiniteLaunchContext({ctx,env,refs:r,parent});check();
+   const material=await readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchMaterial({ctx,env,context,parent});check();
+   const m=fields(material,['ctx','env','plan','request','execution']);
+   if(m.ctx!==ctx||m.env!==env||typeof m.plan!=='string'||typeof m.request!=='function')fail();
+   const rawPlan=JSON.parse(m.plan);validateFiniteLaunchPolicy({T0:rawPlan.T0,launchPolicy:rawPlan.launchPolicy});
+   narrow(operation==='prepare'?rawPlan.launchPolicy.armAckBy:operation==='create'?rawPlan.launchPolicy.lastCreateAt:rawPlan.R1);
+   const nativePin=createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin({ctx,env,sourceRevision:rawPlan.source.revision});
+   const snapshot=async()=>{check();const s=await readPrivateQaFiniteControlSnapshotUnderCoordinator({storage,nativePin,plan:m.plan,parent});check();return s;};
+   const initial=await snapshot(),c=initial.control,p=c.plan,execution=fields(m.execution,['intentId','controlHash','sourceRevision','moduleHash','confirmationId','observerId','admissionHash','launcherId']);
+   if(p.intentId!==r.intentId||(operation==='prepare'?p.source.revision!==r.sourceRevision:c.controlHash!==r.controlHash)||execution.intentId!==p.intentId||execution.controlHash!==c.controlHash||execution.sourceRevision!==p.source.revision||execution.moduleHash!==p.source.moduleHash||execution.confirmationId!==p.workflows.confirmation||execution.observerId!==p.workflows.bootstrap||execution.launcherId!==p.workflows.launcher||!hex(execution.admissionHash))fail();
+   const prefix=JOURNAL+p.intentId+':',coordinator={ctx,env,context,parent};
+   const facts=s=>({prepared:s.prepared,send:s.send,ack:s.ack,nativeCreateAcknowledged:s.ack!==null});
+   if(operation==='read')return result('observed',facts(initial));
+   if(operation==='create'&&initial.send)return result(initial.ack?'acknowledged':'issued',facts(initial));
+   const supplied=await readPrivateQaFiniteLaunchOperations({ctx,env,context,parent});check();
+   const {controlPrimary,guardActions}=fields(supplied,['controlPrimary','guardActions']);
+   const primary=fields(controlPrimary,['admission','finalizer','subscription','process','savedEvent','effect','nominal']);
+   if(Object.values(primary).some(v=>typeof v!=='function'))fail();
+   const actions=fields(guardActions,['readPrimary','withdraw','close','seal','proof','canary_restore']);
+   // The source producer owns real setup/launcher/primary/custody qualification.
+   // Actual fixed enumeration and absence qualification remain part of prepare.
+   const inventory=await createPrivateQaFiniteLaunchInventory(coordinator);check();
+   const readPrimary={...primary,admission:async()=>{
+    check();const observation=await inventory.observeBeforeCreation();check();
+    if(observation.kind!=='never_issued')fail();
+    const value=await primary.admission();check();return value;
+   }};
+   const control=createPrivateQaFiniteTrialControl({storage,nativePin,plan:m.plan,readPrimary,now:check,timeoutMs:Math.max(1,Math.min(b.deadline,phaseEnd)-check())});
+   const eligible=s=>{
+    if(!s.state||s.state.phase!=='armed'||s.state.withdrawnAt!==null||s.cleanupStarted||s.sealed||s.guard?.state!=='armed'||s.guard.deliveries!==0||s.guard.nextAt!==p.C0||s.alarm!==p.C0)fail();
+   };
+   // Compare complete validated commitments INSIDE the append transaction.
+   // Await actual transaction settlement: outer coordinator alone bounds caller.
+   const append=async(kind,record,expected,critical)=>{
+    critical();const status=await transaction.call(storage,async tx=>{
+     critical();
+     const list=async prefix=>{const rows=await tx.list({prefix,limit:65});critical();if(!(rows instanceof Map)||rows.size>64)fail();return rows;};
+     const controlRows=await list(ROOT+':'),guardRows=await list(GUARD),journalRows=await list(JOURNAL);
+     const alarm=await tx.getAlarm();critical();
+     if(digest('afw.qa-finite-control-snapshot.v1',{controlRows:sorted(controlRows),guardRows:sorted(guardRows),alarm})!==expected.controlSnapshotHash||digest('afw.qa-finite-launch-journal-snapshot.v1',sorted(journalRows))!==expected.journalSnapshotHash)fail();
+     if(journalRows.has(prefix+kind))return false;
+     critical();await tx.put(prefix+kind,record);critical();return true;
+    });critical();return status;
+   };
+   if(operation==='prepare'){
+    if(initial.state||initial.prepared||initial.send)fail();
+    // Never hold the SEND coordinator while this control awaits its inventory.
+    const armed=await control.prepare();check();if(armed.status!=='armed')fail();
+    const state=await snapshot();if(state.control.controlHash!==c.controlHash)fail();
+    const guard=createPrivateQaFiniteTrialGuard({ctx,env,control,capabilities:{identity:state.control,...actions},now:check});
+    const guarded=await guard.arm();check();if(guarded.status!=='armed'||guarded.nextAt!==p.C0)fail();
+    const guardedState=await snapshot();eligible(guardedState);
+    const claim=await control.claimEffect('nominal');check();if(claim.status!=='issued'||claim.controlHash!==c.controlHash)fail();
+    return await withPrivateQaFiniteLaunchCoordinator(coordinator,async current=>{
+     const critical=()=>{current();return check();};critical();
+     const state=await snapshot();critical();eligible(state);
+     if(state.prepared||state.send||!same(state.nominalClaim,claim.claim))fail();
+     const prepared=validateFiniteLaunchPrepared({purpose:'afw.qa-finite-launch-prepared.v1',intentId:p.intentId,finitePlanHash:c.planHash,controlHash:c.controlHash,sourceRevision:p.source.revision,qaVersionId:p.deployments.qaLive.versionId,actorId:p.resources.actorId,launcherId:p.workflows.launcher,nominalId:p.workflows.confirmation,bundleHash:p.bundleHash,nominalClaimHash:digest(ROOT+':effect',claim.claim),guardStateHash:hash(state.guard),preparedAt:critical(),...p.launchPolicy});
+     if(!await append('prepared',prepared,state,critical))fail();critical();
+     const readback=await snapshot();critical();eligible(readback);
+     if(!same(readback.prepared,prepared)||!same(readback.nominalClaim,claim.claim))fail();
+     return result('prepared',{prepared:readback.prepared});
+    });
+   }
+   if(check()<p.launchPolicy.launchAt||!initial.prepared)fail();
+   // Full protected source observation before acquiring the SEND slot. All native
+   // create writers share that slot; a competing sender is caught by fresh state.
+   const observation=await inventory.observeBeforeCreation();check();
+   if(observation.kind!=='never_issued'||observation.phase!=='prelaunch')fail();
+   const bindingIdentity={accountId:'85d0d5dadac3341a564f22ce885e9eec',bindingName:'AFW_QA_NAMED_CONFIRMATION',className:'PrivateQaNamedConfirmation',definitionName:'afw-own-qa-private-confirmation',qaVersionId:p.deployments.qaLive.versionId,scriptName:'agent-friendly-web-independent-closure-qa',sourceModuleHash:p.source.moduleHash,sourceProfileHash:p.source.profileHash,sourceRevision:p.source.revision};
+   const bindingHash=digest('afw.finite-native-child-binding.v1',Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(bindingIdentity).sort().map(k=>[k,bindingIdentity[k]])));
+   return await withPrivateQaFiniteLaunchCoordinator(coordinator,async current=>{
+    const critical=()=>{current();const at=check();if(at<p.launchPolicy.launchAt)fail();return at;};
+    const fresh=async()=>{
+     critical();const value=await control.readLaunchControl();critical();
+     if(value.status!=='observed'||value.value.controlHash!==c.controlHash||value.value.claimHash!==initial.prepared.nominalClaimHash)fail();
+     // Retain current consent through every later await, including this storage
+     // snapshot. narrow() only reduces the SAME invocation bound; later primary
+     // evidence cannot widen it or renew the original parent/context.
+     narrow(value.value.consent.expiresAt);
+     const state=await snapshot();critical();eligible(state);
+     if(!same(state.prepared,initial.prepared)||!same(state.nominalClaim,value.value.claim))fail();return state;
+    };
+    let state=await fresh();critical();
+    if(state.send)return result(state.ack?'acknowledged':'issued',facts(state));
+    if(state.controlSnapshotHash!==observation.controlSnapshotHash||state.journalSnapshotHash!==observation.journalSnapshotHash)fail();
+    const prepared=state.prepared;
+    const send=validateFiniteChildCreateSend({purpose:'afw.qa-finite-child-create-send.v1',...Object.fromEntries(['intentId','finitePlanHash','controlHash','sourceRevision','qaVersionId','actorId','launcherId','nominalId','bundleHash','nominalClaimHash'].map(k=>[k,prepared[k]])),preparedHash:hashFiniteLaunchRecord(prepared),bindingHash,issuedAt:critical(),deadline:p.launchPolicy.lastCreateAt});
+    // This local capability exists only after a NEW acknowledged transaction.
+    let once=await append('send',send,state,critical);critical();if(!once)fail();

+    // acknowledgement is relevant. It does not prove execution or termination.
+    if(response?.id!==p.workflows.confirmation)fail();
+    const ack=validateFiniteChildCreateAck({purpose:'afw.qa-finite-child-create-ack.v1',intentId:p.intentId,controlHash:c.controlHash,sendHash:hashFiniteLaunchRecord(send),nominalId:p.workflows.confirmation,acknowledgedAt:critical()});
+    state=await snapshot();critical();eligible(state);if(!same(state.send,send)||state.ack)fail();
+    if(!await append('ack',ack,state,critical))fail();critical();
+    const readback=await snapshot();critical();if(!same(readback.send,send)||!same(readback.ack,ack))fail();
+    return result('acknowledged',facts(readback));

+  return await Promise.race([work(),timeout]);
+ }catch{return result('admin_pending');}

+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launcher.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launcher.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-launcher.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import {finiteLauncherInstanceId,validateFiniteLaunchPrepared,hashFiniteLaunchRecord} from './mcp-qa-finite-launch-records.mjs';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data,finiteSame as same,finiteTime as time} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite launcher unavailable');};
+export const FINITE_LAUNCHER_OPERATION='finite_nominal_launcher';
+const slot='afw-own-successor-closure-v1',name='afw-own-qa-private-bootstrap';
+const stepOptions=Object.freeze({retries:Object.freeze({limit:0,delay:'1 second',backoff:'constant'}),timeout:'5 seconds'});
+const pending=()=>({status:'admin_pending',mayRetry:false,authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false});
+async function metadata(promise){const value=await promise;try{return structuredClone(value);}finally{value?.[Symbol.dispose]?.();}}
+/** Only the finite source class mounts this operation. C2 inside the fixed DO
+ * independently qualifies actual launcher/source/seed/READY and step completion.
+ * Stored metadata and a replayed step result are never a new send capability. */
+export async function runPrivateQaFiniteLauncher({env,event,step}){
+ try{
+  const payload=event.payload,x=fields(payload,['operation','intentId','sourceRevision']),refs={intentId:x.intentId,sourceRevision:x.sourceRevision};
+  const id=finiteLauncherInstanceId(refs),ns=env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,version=env.AFW_QA_VERSION?.id;
+  if(x.operation!==FINITE_LAUNCHER_OPERATION||event.instanceId!==id||event.schedule!==undefined||event.workflowName!==undefined&&event.workflowName!==name||!version||typeof step?.do!=='function'||typeof step?.sleepUntil!=='function')fail();
+  const actorId=ns.idFromName(slot),actor=ns.get(actorId),own=()=>{
+   if(env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE!==ns||env.AFW_QA_VERSION?.id!==version||event.payload!==payload||!same(fields(payload,['operation','intentId','sourceRevision']),x)||event.instanceId!==id||event.schedule!==undefined||event.workflowName!==undefined&&event.workflowName!==name||ns.idFromName(slot).toString()!==actorId.toString())fail();
+  };
+  const validate=record=>{const p=validateFiniteLaunchPrepared(record);if(p.intentId!==x.intentId||p.sourceRevision!==x.sourceRevision||p.launcherId!==id||p.actorId!==actorId.toString()||p.qaVersionId!==version)fail();return p;};
+  let firstExecuted=false;
+  const envelope=fields(await metadata(step.do('finite-launch-prepare',stepOptions,async()=>{
+   firstExecuted=true;own();const began=Date.now(),mono=performance.now();let timer,active=true;
+   const check=()=>{own();const at=Date.now();if(!active||!time(at)||at<began||at-began>=5000||performance.now()-mono>=5000)fail();return at;};
+   try{return await Promise.race([(async()=>{
+    check();const r=fields(await metadata(actor.prepareFiniteNominalLaunch(refs)),['status','prepared','mayRetry','authorizesEffects','operationalReadiness']);check();
+    if(r.status!=='prepared'||r.mayRetry!==false||r.authorizesEffects!==false||r.operationalReadiness!==false)fail();
+    const prepared=validate(r.prepared);if(check()>prepared.armAckBy||began<prepared.armAckBy-90000)fail();
+    const read=await metadata(actor.readFiniteNominalLaunch({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:prepared.controlHash}));check();
+    if(read.status!=='observed'||read.mayRetry!==false||read.authorizesEffects!==false||read.operationalReadiness!==false||!same(validate(read.prepared),prepared)||read.send!==null||read.ack!==null||read.nativeCreateAcknowledged!==false)fail();
+    const acknowledgedAt=check();if(acknowledgedAt>prepared.armAckBy)fail();
+    return {purpose:'afw.finite-launcher-prepared.v1',prepared,preparedHash:hashFiniteLaunchRecord(prepared),acknowledgedAt};
+   })(),new Promise((_,reject)=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>{active=false;reject(Error('Finite launcher unavailable'));},5000);})]);}
+   finally{active=false;clearTimeout(timer);}
+  })),['purpose','prepared','preparedHash','acknowledgedAt']);
+  own();const prepared=validate(envelope.prepared),T0=prepared.armAckBy-90000;
+  if(envelope.purpose!=='afw.finite-launcher-prepared.v1'||envelope.preparedHash!==hashFiniteLaunchRecord(prepared)||!time(envelope.acknowledgedAt)||envelope.acknowledgedAt<prepared.preparedAt||envelope.acknowledgedAt>prepared.armAckBy||firstExecuted&&Date.now()>prepared.armAckBy)fail();
+  let last=T0;const check=()=>{own();const at=Date.now();if(!time(at)||at<last||at<T0||at>=prepared.lastCreateAt)fail();last=at;return at;};
+  check();await step.sleepUntil('finite-launch-wait',new Date(prepared.launchAt));check();
+  return await metadata(step.do('finite-launch-create',stepOptions,async()=>{
+   if(check()<prepared.launchAt)fail();
+   // Actual successful provider prepare-step completion <=armAckBy is C2's
+   // mandatory source qualification inside this one-shot server method.
+   const r=await metadata(actor.createFiniteNominalChild({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:prepared.controlHash}));own();
+   if(!['acknowledged','issued','admin_pending'].includes(r?.status)||r.mayRetry!==false||r.authorizesEffects!==false||r.operationalReadiness!==false)fail();
+   return data(r);
+  }));
+ }catch{return pending();}
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer-native.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer-native.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer-native.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer-native.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+import {projectFiniteExternalPlan,validateFinitePrimaryReceipt} from './mcp-qa-finite-external-contract.mjs';
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteTrialControl,createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin,projectPrivateQaFiniteReferences} from './mcp-qa-finite-trial-control.mjs';
+import {createPrivateQaFiniteCleanupStore,readFiniteCleanupControl} from './mcp-qa-finite-cleanup-store.mjs';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteSame as same,finiteHash as hash,finiteHex as hex,finiteTime as time} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+import {FINITE_OBSERVER_SLOT,validateFiniteMaterialPreview} from './mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs';
+let factory;
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite native material unavailable');};
+/** Task4 installs actual compiled producer. Absence is unavailable, never env DTO. */
+export function installPrivateQaFiniteMaterialFactory(value){if(factory||typeof value!=='function')fail();factory=value;}
+async function resolve(ctx,env,refs){
+ if(!factory||ctx.id.toString()!==env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE.idFromName(FINITE_OBSERVER_SLOT).toString())fail();
+ const capabilities=fields(await factory({ctx,env,refs}),['controlOptions','readFinalPrimary','custody']),options=capabilities.controlOptions;
+ if(options?.storage!==ctx.storage)fail();
+ createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin({ctx,env,sourceRevision:refs.sourceRevision});
+ return {options,capabilities,control:createPrivateQaFiniteTrialControl(options)};
+}
+export async function previewPrivateQaFiniteTrialMaterial({ctx,env,refs}={}){
+ const x=fields(refs,['intentId','sourceRevision']);if(!hex(x.intentId)||!hex(x.sourceRevision,40))fail();
+ const {control}=await resolve(ctx,env,x),p=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(await control.preview());
+ if(p.intentId!==x.intentId||p.sourceRevision!==x.sourceRevision)fail();return p;
+}
+/** The single explicit journal mutation in the observer. Existing/uncertain
+ * native claim is never upgraded to an acknowledged handoff on reconstruction. */
+export async function claimPrivateQaFiniteTrialRestore({ctx,env,refs,now=Date.now}={}){
+ const x=fields(refs,['intentId','controlHash','proofHash']);if(!hex(x.intentId)||!hex(x.controlHash)||!hex(x.proofHash))fail();
+ const entered=now(),mono=performance.now();let last=entered,done=false,timer;
+ const check=()=>{const n=now();if(done||!time(n)||n<last||n-entered>=5000||performance.now()-mono>=5000)fail();last=n;return n;};
+ const work=async()=>{
+  check();const c=await readFiniteCleanupControl({storage:ctx.storage,intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:x.controlHash});check();
+  const p=c.plan;if(check()<p.C0||check()>=p.R1)fail();
+  const store=createPrivateQaFiniteCleanupStore({storage:ctx.storage,control:c,now:check});
+  const r=await store.read();check();const completion=await store.completion();check();
+  if(r.status!=='observed'||r.record.proofHash!==x.proofHash||completion.status!=='complete'||completion.receipt.proofHash!==x.proofHash)fail();
+  const {options,control}=await resolve(ctx,env,{intentId:x.intentId,sourceRevision:p.source.revision});check();
+  if(options.plan!==JSON.stringify(p))fail();
+  // control.claimEffect invokes the actual source producer's fresh qa_restore
+  // eligibility (native pins + admitted finalizer), never an RPC boolean.
+  const claimed=await control.claimEffect('qa_restore');check();
+  if(claimed.status!=='issued'||claimed.controlHash!==x.controlHash||claimed.claim?.proofHash!==x.proofHash||claimed.claim?.deadline!==p.R1||check()>=p.R1)fail();
+  const retained=await store.read(),completed=await store.completion();check();
+  if(!same(retained.record,r.record)||!same(completed.receipt,completion.receipt))fail();
+  return Object.freeze({purpose:'afw.finite-qa-claim-handoff.v1',claim:claimed.claim,claimHash:hash(claimed.claim)});
+ };
+ try{return await Promise.race([work(),new Promise((_,reject)=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>{done=true;reject(Error('Finite claim unavailable'));},5000);})]);}finally{done=true;clearTimeout(timer);}
+}
+
+/** One final native readonly first-primary receipt, not an external D1 API.
+ * The source-qualified Task4 producer must enumerate held remote writers too. */
+export async function readPrivateQaFiniteFinalPrimary({ctx,env,refs,now=Date.now}={}){
+ const x=fields(refs,['intentId','controlHash','proofHash','claimHash']);
+ if(!hex(x.intentId)||['controlHash','proofHash','claimHash'].some(k=>!hex(x[k])))fail();
+ const entered=now(),mono=performance.now();let last=entered,done=false,timer;
+ const check=()=>{const n=now();if(done||!time(n)||n<last||n-entered>=5000||performance.now()-mono>=5000)fail();last=n;return n;};
+ const work=async()=>{
+  const c=await readFiniteCleanupControl({storage:ctx.storage,intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:x.controlHash});check();const p=c.plan;
+  if(check()>=p.R1)fail();
+  const {options,capabilities}=await resolve(ctx,env,{intentId:x.intentId,sourceRevision:p.source.revision});check();
+  if(options.plan!==JSON.stringify(p)||typeof capabilities.readFinalPrimary!=='function')fail();
+  const store=createPrivateQaFiniteCleanupStore({storage:ctx.storage,control:c,now:check});
+  const read=async()=>{
+   const retained=await store.read(),completion=await store.completion(),claim=await ctx.storage.get('afw.qa-finite-trial.v1:'+p.intentId+':effect:qa_restore');check();
+   if(retained.status!=='observed'||completion.status!=='complete'||retained.record.proofHash!==x.proofHash||hash(claim)!==x.claimHash||claim.kind!=='qa_restore'||claim.proofHash!==x.proofHash||claim.controlHash!==x.controlHash)fail();
+   return {retained:retained.record,completion:completion.receipt,claim};
+  };
+  const before=await read();check();
+  const observed=fields(await capabilities.readFinalPrimary(check),['primary','writers']);check();
+  const after=await read();check();
+  createPrivateQaFiniteTrialNativePin({ctx,env,sourceRevision:p.source.revision});check();
+  if(!same(before,after)||check()>=p.R1)fail();
+  const plan=projectFiniteExternalPlan({control:c,preview:projectPrivateQaFiniteReferences(c),custody:capabilities.custody});
+  const body={purpose:'afw.finite-final-primary.v1',intentId:p.intentId,controlHash:c.controlHash,planHash:c.planHash,proofHash:x.proofHash,completionHash:before.completion.receiptHash,nativeClaimHash:x.claimHash,sealHash:before.retained.proof.sealHash,sourceRevision:p.source.revision,runtimeVersionId:p.deployments.qaLive.versionId,actorId:p.resources.actorId,closedCanaryVersionId:p.deployments.canaryClosed.versionId,closedCanaryModuleHash:p.deployments.canaryClosed.module.sha256,primary:observed.primary,writers:observed.writers,observedAt:check()};
+  const receipt={...body,receiptHash:hash(body)};
+  validateFinitePrimaryReceipt(receipt,plan,before.retained,before.completion,{claim:before.claim,claimHash:x.claimHash});
+  return {plan,receipt};
+ };
+ try{return await Promise.race([work(),new Promise((_,reject)=>{timer=setTimeout(()=>{done=true;reject(Error('Finite primary unavailable'));},5000);})]);}finally{done=true;clearTimeout(timer);}
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-observer.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+import {finiteFields as fields,finiteData as data,finiteHex as hex,finiteTime as time,finiteHash as hash} from './mcp-finite-evidence.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite observer unavailable');};
+export const FINITE_OBSERVER_OPERATION='finite_cleanup_observer';
+export const FINITE_PREVIEW_STEP='finite-material-preview';
+export const FINITE_CLAIM_STEP='finite-qa-restore-claim';
+export const FINITE_OBSERVER_SLOT='afw-own-successor-closure-v1';
+let compiledSource=null;
+export function installPrivateQaFiniteObserverSource(revision){if(compiledSource!==null||!hex(revision,40))fail();compiledSource=revision;}
+// Native RPC adds a disposable transport handle to plain result metadata.
+// Clone only the serializable DTO, release the handle, then validate strictly.
+async function metadata(promise){const value=await promise;try{return structuredClone(value);}finally{value?.[Symbol.dispose]?.();}}
+const stepOptions=Object.freeze({retries:Object.freeze({limit:0,delay:'1 second',backoff:'constant'}),timeout:'5 seconds'});
+export function finiteObserverInstanceId(refs){
+ const x=fields(refs,['intentId','sourceRevision']);if(!hex(x.intentId)||!hex(x.sourceRevision,40))fail();
+ return 'afw-finite-observer-'+createHash('sha256').update('afw.finite-observer.v1\n'+x.intentId+'\n'+x.sourceRevision).digest('hex');
+}
+export function validateFiniteMaterialPreview(value){
+ const p=fields(data(value),['purpose','intentId','sourceRevision','runtimeVersionId','actorId','planHash','controlHash','resourceHash','T0','N1','C0','C1','R1']);
+ if(p.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-material-preview.v1'||!hex(p.intentId)||!hex(p.sourceRevision,40)||!(/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/).test(p.runtimeVersionId)||['actorId','planHash','controlHash','resourceHash'].some(k=>!hex(p[k]))||!time(p.T0)||p.N1!==p.T0+300000||p.C0!==p.N1-60000||p.C1!==p.N1+120000||p.R1!==p.N1+180000)fail();return p;
+}
+export function validateFiniteQaClaim(value,preview,proofHash){
+ const p=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(preview),r=fields(data(value),['purpose','claim','claimHash']),c=fields(r.claim,['purpose','intentId','controlHash','kind','phase','issuedAt','deadline','proofHash']);
+ if(r.purpose!=='afw.finite-qa-claim-handoff.v1'||c.purpose!=='afw.qa-finite-trial.v1:effect'||c.intentId!==p.intentId||c.controlHash!==p.controlHash||c.kind!=='qa_restore'||c.phase!=='issued'||c.proofHash!==proofHash||!hex(proofHash)||!time(c.issuedAt)||c.issuedAt<p.C0||c.issuedAt>=p.R1||c.deadline!==p.R1||r.claimHash!==hash(c))fail();return r;
+}
+/** One native Workflow. No public HTTP or caller-selected actor. Its FIRST step
+ * is read-only preview; exactly one separately named journal claim follows
+ * settled cleanup. Distinct observation step IDs avoid caching absent forever. */
+export async function runPrivateQaFiniteObserver({env,event,step,now=Date.now}={}){
+ try{
+  const x=fields(data(event.payload),['operation','intentId','sourceRevision']),refs={intentId:x.intentId,sourceRevision:x.sourceRevision};
+  if(x.operation!==FINITE_OBSERVER_OPERATION||event.instanceId!==finiteObserverInstanceId(refs)||typeof step?.do!=='function'||typeof step?.sleepUntil!=='function'||typeof now!=='function')fail();
+  if(compiledSource!==x.sourceRevision||!env.AFW_QA_VERSION?.id)fail();
+  const ownVersion=env.AFW_QA_VERSION.id,provenance={purpose:'afw.finite-observer-provenance.v1',instanceId:event.instanceId,sourceRevision:compiledSource,runtimeVersionId:ownVersion};
+  const own=()=>{if(compiledSource!==x.sourceRevision||env.AFW_QA_VERSION?.id!==ownVersion)fail();};
+  const ns=env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,actor=ns.get(ns.idFromName(FINITE_OBSERVER_SLOT));
+  const envelope=await metadata(step.do(FINITE_PREVIEW_STEP,stepOptions,async()=>{own();const material=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(await metadata(actor.previewFiniteTrialMaterial(refs)));own();return data({purpose:'afw.finite-observer-preview.v1',provenance,material});}));
+  if(envelope.purpose!=='afw.finite-observer-preview.v1'||JSON.stringify(envelope.provenance)!==JSON.stringify(provenance))fail();
+  const preview=validateFiniteMaterialPreview(envelope.material);
+  if(preview.intentId!==x.intentId||preview.sourceRevision!==x.sourceRevision||preview.actorId!==ns.idFromName(FINITE_OBSERVER_SLOT).toString()||env.AFW_QA_VERSION?.id!==preview.runtimeVersionId)fail();
+  let last=preview.T0;const check=()=>{own();const at=now();if(!time(at)||at<last||at<preview.T0||at>=preview.R1)fail();last=at;return at;};
+  // Preview + at most 30 samples + final-primary = at most 32 readonly steps.
+  for(let i=0;i<30;i++){
+   check();const target=preview.C0+Math.floor(i*(preview.R1-preview.C0-5000)/29);
+   if(check()<target)await step.sleepUntil('finite-observe-wait-'+i,new Date(target));check();
+   const result=await metadata(step.do('finite-cleanup-observation-'+i,stepOptions,async()=>{
+    check();const head=await metadata(actor.readFiniteTrialCleanupProofHead({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:preview.controlHash}));check();
+    if(head.status==='not_yet_observed')return {status:'not_yet_observed'};
+    if(head.status!=='observed'||head.intentId!==x.intentId||head.controlHash!==preview.controlHash||!hex(head.proofHash))fail();
+    const retained=await metadata(actor.readRetainedFiniteCleanupProof({intentId:x.intentId,proofHash:head.proofHash}));check();
+    const completion=await metadata(actor.readFiniteTrialCleanupCompletion({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:preview.controlHash,proofHash:head.proofHash}));check();
+    if(completion.status==='not_yet_observed')return {status:'not_yet_observed'};
+    if(retained.status!=='observed'||completion.status!=='complete'||retained.record?.proofHash!==head.proofHash||completion.receipt?.proofHash!==head.proofHash||completion.receipt?.controlHash!==preview.controlHash)fail();
+    return data({status:'observed',retained:retained.record,completion:completion.receipt});
+   }));check();
+   if(result.status==='not_yet_observed')continue;
+   const qaClaim=validateFiniteQaClaim(await metadata(step.do(FINITE_CLAIM_STEP,stepOptions,()=>metadata(actor.claimFiniteTrialQaRestore({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:preview.controlHash,proofHash:result.retained.proofHash})))),preview,result.retained.proofHash);check();
+   if(qaClaim.claim.issuedAt<result.completion.completedAt)fail();
+   const finalPrimary=await metadata(step.do('finite-final-primary',stepOptions,()=>metadata(actor.readFiniteTrialFinalPrimary({intentId:x.intentId,controlHash:preview.controlHash,proofHash:result.retained.proofHash,claimHash:qaClaim.claimHash}))));check();
+   return data({purpose:'afw.finite-observer-output.v1',instanceId:event.instanceId,provenance,preview,retained:result.retained,completion:result.completion,qaClaim,plan:finalPrimary.plan,primaryReceipt:finalPrimary.receipt});
+  }
+ }catch{/* Sanitized: unavailable provenance/custody/ACK never fabricates output. */}
+ return Object.freeze({status:'admin_pending',authorizesEffects:false,operationalReadiness:false,mayRetry:false});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-approved-ticket-facts.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-approved-ticket-facts.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-approved-ticket-facts.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-approved-ticket-facts.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {hashFiniteOwnerEvidence as evidence,hashFiniteOwnerPreparationProject as projectHash,hashFinitePreparationRecord as hash,encodeFinitePreparationRecord as encode,decodeFinitePreparationRecord as decode} from './mcp-qa-finite-preparation-records.mjs';
+import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
+const fail=()=>{const e=Error('Finite owner preparation unavailable');e.status=403;throw e;};
+const failPair=()=>{const e=Error('Finite owner preparation unavailable');e.status=503;throw e;};
+const sha=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
+/** Pure predicates; actual owner verification, clocks and all I/O stay with callers. */
+export function qualifyFiniteOwnerApprovedTicket(input){
+ const v=own(input);fields(v,['source','identity','currentTicket','ticket','use','pre','approval','snapshot','realm','subjectHash','observedAt']);
+ const {source,identity,currentTicket,ticket,use,pre,approval,snapshot,realm,subjectHash,observedAt}=v,id=currentTicket.preconnectionId;
+ const decision=evidence('decision',{ticket,decision:'approve',projectHash:projectHash(source.project),goal:source.goal,clientId:source.clientId,approvedAt:approval.approvedAt,expiresAt:approval.expiresAt});
+ if(ticket.preconnectionId!==id||ticket.projectRef!==currentTicket.projectRef||ticket.subjectHash!==subjectHash||use.recordHash!==decision||approval.csrfReceiptHash!==decision||hash('ConfigurationApproval',approval)!==pre.approvalHash||approval.subjectHash!==subjectHash||approval.sourceRevision!==source.sourceRevision||approval.projectHash!==projectHash(source.project)||approval.clientId!==source.clientId||pre.owner.subject!==identity.subject||pre.owner.accessRealmHash!==realm||pre.sourceRevision!==source.sourceRevision||pre.actorId!==source.actorId||pre.clientId!==source.clientId||pre.resource!==source.resource||projectHash(pre.project)!==projectHash(source.project)||pre.baselineHash!==evidence('baseline',snapshot.source.initialBaseline)||!Number.isSafeInteger(observedAt)||observedAt>=Math.min(pre.expiresAt,pre.owner.accessExpiresAt,approval.expiresAt))fail();
+}
+const data=(kind,input)=>decode(kind,encode(kind,own(input)));
+export const finiteOwnerAuthorizationLink=input=>data('AuthorizationLink',input);
+export const finiteOwnerGrantLink=input=>data('GrantLink',input);
+export const finiteOwnerPreconnectionConnection=input=>data('PreconnectionConnection',input);
+export const finiteOwnerConnectionRef=input=>data('ConnectionRef',input);
+export function qualifyFiniteOwnerConnectionPair(input){
+ const v=own(input);fields(v,['source','identity','pre','link','grant','relation','connection','observedAt']);
+ const {source,identity,pre,link,grant,observedAt}=v,relation=finiteOwnerPreconnectionConnection(v.relation),connection=finiteOwnerConnectionRef(v.connection);
+ if(relation.preconnectionId!==pre.preconnectionId||relation.grantHash!==sha(grant.grantId)||relation.grantHash!==link.grantHash||relation.linkedAt<link.acceptedAt||relation.linkedAt>observedAt||connection.connectionRef!==relation.connectionRef||connection.issuedAt!==relation.linkedAt||connection.subject!==identity.subject||connection.grantId!==grant.grantId||connection.clientId!==source.clientId||connection.projectId!==source.project.projectId||connection.resource!==source.resource||connection.expiresAt>Math.min(pre.expiresAt,pre.owner.accessExpiresAt,Date.parse(grant.expiresAt))||!Number.isSafeInteger(observedAt)||observedAt>=connection.expiresAt)failPair();
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-http.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-http.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-http.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-http.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+import {createFiniteOwnerRequestLease} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs';
+import {observeFiniteOwnerRequestWork} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+const headers={'Cache-Control':'no-store','Content-Type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','Referrer-Policy':'no-referrer','X-Content-Type-Options':'nosniff','Content-Security-Policy':"default-src 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'"};
+const pending='<h1>Preparación pendiente</h1><p>Esta preparación todavía no está disponible.</p>';
+/** UNMOUNTED. Cleanup handoff requires separate qualification before any choice,
+ * ticket consumption or provider publication can be exposed on this host. */
+export function createFiniteOwnerPreparationHost({ctx,env}){
+ return Object.freeze({async fetch(request){
+  const parent=createFiniteOwnerRequestLease(request);
+  try{return await observeFiniteOwnerRequestWork({ctx,env,originalLease:parent.lease},async()=>new Response(pending,{status:503,headers}));}
+  catch{return new Response(pending,{status:503,headers});}
+  finally{parent.finish();}
+ }});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-journal.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-journal.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-journal.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-journal.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialHex as hex} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {captureFinitePreparationIssuerContext} from './mcp-qa-finite-preparation-issuer-journal.mjs';
+import {FINITE_OWNER_ISSUER_PROVENANCE_PREFIX as P,FINITE_OWNER_SDK_OPERATIONS as OPS,encodeFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceRecord as encode,decodeFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceRecord as decode} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-records.mjs';
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite owner issuer provenance unavailable');};
+export function createFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceJournal(options){
+ fields(options,['ctx','env']);const c=captureFinitePreparationIssuerContext(options);
+ const prefix=id=>{if(!hex(id))fail();return P+id+':';};
+ const record=(kind,value)=>decode(kind,encode(kind,value));
+ async function inventory(id,guard,storage=c.storage){
+  const list=storage.list;if(typeof list!=='function')fail();guard();
+  const rows=await c.wait(Reflect.apply(list,storage,[{prefix:prefix(id),limit:9}]),guard);
+  if(!(rows instanceof Map)||rows.size>8)fail();
+  const out={root:null,grant:null,operations:Object.fromEntries(OPS.map(op=>[op,{send:null,observed:null}]))},entries=[];
+  for(const [key,raw]of rows){
+   const suffix=key.slice(prefix(id).length);let kind,target,field;
+   if(suffix==='root'||suffix==='grant'){kind=suffix==='root'?'Root':'ExpectedGrant';target=out;field=suffix;}
+   else{const parts=suffix.split(':');if(parts.length!==2||!OPS.includes(parts[0])||!['send','observed'].includes(parts[1]))fail();kind=parts[1]==='send'?'SdkSend':'SdkObserved';target=out.operations[parts[0]];field=parts[1];}
+   target[field]=decode(kind,raw);entries.push(Object.freeze([key,raw]));
+  }
+  const root=out.root;
+  if(!root){if(rows.size)fail();}
+  else{
+   if(root.preconnectionId!==id||root.actorId!==c.actorId||root.qaVersionId!==c.qa)fail();
+   for(const op of OPS){
+    const {send,observed}=out.operations[op];
+    if(send&&(send.rootHash!==root.recordHash||send.operation!==op||send.issuedAt<root.createdAt||send.deadline>root.C0))fail();
+    if(observed&&(!send||observed.sendHash!==send.recordHash||observed.observedAt<send.issuedAt||observed.observedAt>=send.deadline))fail();
+   }
+   const begin=out.operations.sdk_begin,approve=out.operations.sdk_approve,complete=out.operations.sdk_complete;
+   if(approve.send&&!begin.observed||out.grant&&(!approve.observed||out.grant.rootHash!==root.recordHash||Date.parse(out.grant.createdAt)<approve.observed.observedAt||Date.parse(out.grant.expiresAt)>root.C0)||complete.send&&(!out.grant||complete.send.bindingHash!==out.grant.recordHash))fail();
+  }
+  for(const op of OPS)Object.freeze(out.operations[op]);Object.freeze(out.operations);
+  return Object.freeze({...out,entries:Object.freeze(entries.sort(([a],[b])=>a.localeCompare(b)))});
+ }
+ async function append(id,suffix,kind,input,lease){
+  const value=record(kind,input),raw=encode(kind,value),guard=c.leaseGuard(lease);guard();
+  try{
+   const status=await c.wait(Reflect.apply(c.transaction,c.storage,[async tx=>{
+    const get=tx.get,put=tx.put,list=tx.list;if([get,put,list].some(x=>typeof x!=='function'))fail();
+    const check=()=>{guard();if(tx.get!==get||tx.put!==put||tx.list!==list)fail();};
+    const before=await inventory(id,check,tx),root=before.root;
+    const old=await c.wait(Reflect.apply(get,tx,[prefix(id)+suffix]),check);
+    if(old!==undefined){if(old!==raw)fail();return 'existing';}
+    if(before.entries.length>=8)fail();
+    if(kind==='Root'){if(root||value.preconnectionId!==id||value.actorId!==c.actorId||value.qaVersionId!==c.qa)fail();}
+    else{
+     if(!root)fail();
+     if(kind==='SdkSend'){
+      if(value.rootHash!==root.recordHash||value.operation+':send'!==suffix||value.issuedAt<root.createdAt||value.deadline>root.C0)fail();
+      if(value.operation==='sdk_approve'&&!before.operations.sdk_begin.observed||value.operation==='sdk_complete'&&(!before.grant||value.bindingHash!==before.grant.recordHash))fail();
+     }
+     if(kind==='SdkObserved'){
+      const send=before.operations[suffix.split(':')[0]]?.send;
+      if(!send||value.sendHash!==send.recordHash||value.observedAt<send.issuedAt||value.observedAt>=send.deadline)fail();
+     }
+     if(kind==='ExpectedGrant'&&(!before.operations.sdk_approve.observed||value.rootHash!==root.recordHash||Date.parse(value.createdAt)<before.operations.sdk_approve.observed.observedAt||Date.parse(value.expiresAt)>root.C0))fail();
+    }
+    check();await c.wait(Reflect.apply(put,tx,[prefix(id)+suffix,raw]),check);return 'recorded';
+   }]),guard);
+   await c.wait(Reflect.apply(c.sync,c.storage,[]),guard);
+   if(await c.wait(Reflect.apply(c.get,c.storage,[prefix(id)+suffix]),guard)!==raw)fail();
+   return Object.freeze({status,recordHash:value.recordHash});
+  }catch{return Object.freeze({status:'uncertain',recordHash:value.recordHash});}
+ }
+ return Object.freeze({
+  read(input,lease){const v=own(input);fields(v,['preconnectionId']);return inventory(v.preconnectionId,c.leaseGuard(lease));},
+  appendRoot(input,lease){const r=record('Root',input);return append(r.preconnectionId,'root','Root',r,lease);},
+  appendSend(input,lease){const v=own(input);fields(v,['preconnectionId','record']);const r=record('SdkSend',v.record);return append(v.preconnectionId,r.operation+':send','SdkSend',r,lease);},
+  appendObserved(input,lease){const v=own(input);fields(v,['preconnectionId','operation','record']);if(!OPS.includes(v.operation))fail();return append(v.preconnectionId,v.operation+':observed','SdkObserved',v.record,lease);},
+  appendExpectedGrant(input,lease){const v=own(input);fields(v,['preconnectionId','record']);return append(v.preconnectionId,'grant','ExpectedGrant',v.record,lease);}
+ });
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-records.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-records.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-records.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-issuer-provenance-records.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import {initialOwn as own,initialFields as fields,initialCanonical as canonical,initialHash as hash,initialHex as hex} from './mcp-qa-finite-initial-material-records.mjs';
+import {issuerTime,issuerUuid,issuerScopes,issuerIso} from './mcp-qa-finite-preparation-issuer-records.mjs';
+// Canonical DATA commitments only. No record asserts caller identity or ownership.
+export const FINITE_OWNER_ISSUER_PROVENANCE_PREFIX='afw.finite-owner-issuer-provenance.v1:';
+export const FINITE_OWNER_SDK_OPERATIONS=Object.freeze(['sdk_begin','sdk_approve','sdk_complete']);
+const defs={
+ Root:['preconnectionId','capsuleHash','enrollmentHash','sourceRevision','actorId','qaVersionId','mVersionId','originalConnectionReceiptHash','I0','C0','C1','createdAt'],
+ SdkSend:['rootHash','operation','originalRequestId','originalRequestHash','issuedAt','deadline','bindingHash'],
+ SdkObserved:['sendHash','resultHash','observedAt','outcome'],
+ ExpectedGrant:['rootHash','identityHash','reservationHash','authorizationHash','grantId','approvedRequestHash','scopes','createdAt','expiresAt']
+};
+const purposes={Root:'afw.finite-owner-issuer-root.v1',SdkSend:'afw.finite-owner-sdk-send.v1',SdkObserved:'afw.finite-owner-sdk-observed.v1',ExpectedGrant:'afw.finite-owner-expected-grant.v1'};
+const fail=()=>{throw Error('Finite owner issuer provenance unavailable');};
+const check=v=>{if(!v)fail();};
+function validate(kind,input,sealing=false){
+ check(Object.hasOwn(defs,kind));
+ const v=own(input);fields(v,['purpose',...defs[kind],...(!sealing||Object.hasOwn(v,'recordHash')?['recordHash']:[])]);
+ check(v.purpose===purposes[kind]&&Buffer.byteLength(canonical(v))<=16384);
+ for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(v))if(key.endsWith('Hash'))check(hex(value));
+ if(kind==='Root'){
+  check(hex(v.preconnectionId)&&hex(v.actorId)&&hex(v.sourceRevision,40)&&issuerUuid(v.qaVersionId)&&issuerUuid(v.mVersionId));
+  check(['I0','C0','C1','createdAt'].every(k=>issuerTime(v[k]))&&v.C0===v.I0+900000&&v.C1===v.C0+180000&&v.createdAt>=v.I0&&v.createdAt<v.C0);
+ }
+ if(kind==='SdkSend')check(FINITE_OWNER_SDK_OPERATIONS.includes(v.operation)&&issuerUuid(v.originalRequestId)&&issuerTime(v.issuedAt)&&issuerTime(v.deadline)&&v.issuedAt<v.deadline&&v.deadline-v.issuedAt<=30000);
+ if(kind==='SdkObserved')check(v.outcome==='resolved'&&issuerTime(v.observedAt));
+ if(kind==='ExpectedGrant')check(issuerUuid(v.grantId)&&issuerScopes(v.scopes)&&issuerIso(v.createdAt)&&issuerIso(v.expiresAt)&&Date.parse(v.createdAt)<Date.parse(v.expiresAt));
+ const body=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k])=>k!=='recordHash'));
+ if(!sealing)check(v.recordHash===hash(v.purpose,body));
+ return v;
+}
+export function sealFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceRecord(kind,input){
+ const v=validate(kind,input,true),body=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k])=>k!=='recordHash'));
+ return validate(kind,{...body,recordHash:hash(v.purpose,body)});
+}
+export function encodeFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceRecord(kind,input){
+ const raw=canonical(validate(kind,input));check(Buffer.byteLength(raw)<=16384);return raw;
+}
+export function decodeFiniteOwnerIssuerProvenanceRecord(kind,raw){
+ check(typeof raw==='string'&&Buffer.byteLength(raw)<=16384);const v=validate(kind,JSON.parse(raw));check(canonical(v)===raw);return v;
+}
~~~~~~~~~~
