## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-bridge.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-bridge.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-bridge.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-bridge.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,45 @@
+import {readFiniteOwnerRequestContext,readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs';
+import {observeFiniteOwnerRequestWork} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+import {finiteNativeOriginalUrl,finiteNativeOrigin,finiteNativeTarget,finiteNativeActor,finiteNativePreservedHeaders,finiteNativeUuid,finiteNativeMetadata,finiteNativeFailure as fail,finiteNativePending as pending,finiteNativeOperation,finiteNativeBytes,finiteNativeCancel,qualifyFiniteOwnerChoiceRequest,finiteNativeChoiceHtml} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs';
+import {readFiniteOwnerNoticeConsentBody as readChoicePostBody} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-notice-consent-body.mjs';
+/** Fixed finite SOURCE transport. No QA holders or business authority in M. */
+export async function forwardFiniteOwnerChoice({request,env,ctx},originalLease){
+ try{
+  if(!(request instanceof Request)||request.url!==finiteNativeOriginalUrl)fail(404);
+  if(!['GET','POST'].includes(request.method))fail(405);
+  if(request.method==='POST'&&request.headers.get('Origin')!==finiteNativeOrigin)fail(403);
+  if(readFiniteOwnerRequestContext(originalLease).root!==originalLease)fail();
+  const originalHeaders=request.headers,headerBytes=JSON.stringify([...originalHeaders]),originalSignal=request.signal,originalUrl=request.url,originalMethod=request.method;
+  const namespace=env?.AFW_FINITE_TRIAL_CONTROL,idFromName=namespace?.idFromName,get=namespace?.get,metadata=env?.AFW_FINITE_TRIAL_VERSION,version=metadata?.id;
+  const db=env?.DB,prepare=db?.prepare,withSession=db?.withSession,kv=env?.OAUTH_KV,kvGet=kv?.get,kvPut=kv?.put,limiter=env?.DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER,limit=limiter?.limit;
+  if(!ctx||[idFromName,get,prepare,withSession,kvGet,kvPut,limit].some(value=>typeof value!=='function')||!finiteNativeUuid(version))fail();
+  const id=Reflect.apply(idFromName,namespace,[finiteNativeActor]),actor=id.toString(),stub=Reflect.apply(get,namespace,[id]),fetcher=stub?.fetch;
+  if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(actor)||typeof fetcher!=='function')fail();
+  let failed=false;const check=()=>{
+   if(failed)fail();originalLease.assertCurrent();
+   if(request.headers!==originalHeaders||JSON.stringify([...originalHeaders])!==headerBytes||request.signal!==originalSignal||request.url!==originalUrl||request.method!==originalMethod||env.AFW_FINITE_TRIAL_CONTROL!==namespace||namespace.idFromName!==idFromName||namespace.get!==get||Reflect.apply(idFromName,namespace,[finiteNativeActor]).toString()!==actor||stub.fetch!==fetcher||
+    env.AFW_FINITE_TRIAL_VERSION!==metadata||metadata.id!==version||env.DB!==db||db.prepare!==prepare||db.withSession!==withSession||env.OAUTH_KV!==kv||kv.get!==kvGet||kv.put!==kvPut||env.DELEGATED_RATE_LIMITER!==limiter||limiter.limit!==limit)fail();
+  };
+  check();
+  return await observeFiniteOwnerRequestWork({ctx,env,originalLease},()=>finiteNativeOperation(originalLease,ctx,async signalFailure=>{
+   const first=error=>{failed=true;signalFailure(error);};
+   const body=await (request.method==='POST'?readChoicePostBody:finiteNativeBytes)(request,check,first);check();
+   qualifyFiniteOwnerChoiceRequest(request,body);
+   // Start with an empty set: every caller private header and arbitrary header
+   // is discarded. Only four future-verifier inputs cross this fixed boundary.
+   const headers=new Headers();for(const name of finiteNativePreservedHeaders){const value=request.headers.get(name);if(value!==null)headers.set(name,value);}
+   headers.set('x-afw-finite-boundary',JSON.stringify(readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary(originalLease)));
+   headers.set('x-afw-finite-original-method',request.method);headers.set('x-afw-finite-original-url',finiteNativeOriginalUrl);
+   headers.set('x-afw-finite-version',version);headers.set('x-afw-finite-request-id',crypto.randomUUID());
+   finiteNativeMetadata(headers);check();
+   const forwarded=new Request(finiteNativeTarget,{method:'POST',redirect:'manual',headers,body:body.length?body:undefined,signal:originalLease.signal});
+   const response=await Reflect.apply(fetcher,stub,[forwarded]);
+   // A known HTTP error wins while its same actual cancellation stays retained.
+   if(!response.ok){try{fail([400,401,403,404,405,413,429].includes(response.status)?response.status:503);}catch(error){return finiteNativeCancel(response,error,first);}}
+   const responseHeaders=response.headers,responseHeaderBytes=JSON.stringify([...responseHeaders]),responseBody=response.body;
+   const responseCheck=()=>{check();if(response.headers!==responseHeaders||JSON.stringify([...responseHeaders])!==responseHeaderBytes||response.body!==responseBody)fail();};
+   const bytes=await finiteNativeBytes(response,responseCheck,first,503);responseCheck();
+   return finiteNativeChoiceHtml(response,bytes);
+  }));
+ }catch(error){return pending([400,401,403,404,405,413,429].includes(error?.status)?error.status:503);}
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-receiver.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-receiver.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-receiver.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-receiver.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,29 @@
+import {handlePrivateQaFiniteOwnerConnection} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-phase-context.mjs';
+import {createFiniteOwnerForwardedRequestLease} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs';
+import {finiteNativeTarget,finiteNativeActor,finiteNativeMetadata,finiteNativeUuid,finiteNativeFailure as fail,finiteNativePending as pending} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs';
+/** Fixed SOURCE receiver. Transport metadata never substitutes for owner proof. */
+export function createFiniteOwnerNativeReceiver({ctx,env}){
+ const storage=ctx?.storage,get=storage?.get,transaction=storage?.transaction,actor=ctx?.id,toString=actor?.toString;
+ const self=env?.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE,idFromName=self?.idFromName,namespaceGet=self?.get;
+ const db=env?.RELEASE_DB,prepare=db?.prepare,withSession=db?.withSession,metadata=env?.AFW_QA_VERSION,version=metadata?.id;
+ return Object.freeze({async fetch(request){
+  let parent;
+  try{
+   if(!(request instanceof Request)||request.url!==finiteNativeTarget)fail(404);
+   if(request.method!=='POST')fail(405);
+   const originalHeaders=request.headers,headerBytes=JSON.stringify([...originalHeaders]),originalSignal=request.signal;
+   const envelope=finiteNativeMetadata(originalHeaders);
+   // Establish the receiving LOCAL root before all body/storage/source I/O.
+   parent=createFiniteOwnerForwardedRequestLease(request,envelope.boundary);
+   let failed=false;const check=()=>{
+    if(failed)fail();parent.lease.assertCurrent();
+    if(request.url!==finiteNativeTarget||request.method!=='POST'||request.headers!==originalHeaders||JSON.stringify([...originalHeaders])!==headerBytes||request.signal!==originalSignal||[get,transaction,toString,idFromName,namespaceGet,prepare,withSession].some(value=>typeof value!=='function')||!finiteNativeUuid(version)||db===env.DB||
+     ctx.storage!==storage||storage.get!==get||storage.transaction!==transaction||ctx.id!==actor||actor.toString!==toString||env.AFW_INDEPENDENT_CLOSURE!==self||self.idFromName!==idFromName||self.get!==namespaceGet||
+     Reflect.apply(toString,actor,[])!==Reflect.apply(idFromName,self,[finiteNativeActor]).toString()||env.RELEASE_DB!==db||db.prepare!==prepare||db.withSession!==withSession||env.AFW_QA_VERSION!==metadata||metadata.id!==version)fail();
+   };
+   check();
+   return await handlePrivateQaFiniteOwnerConnection({request,env,ctx},parent.lease);
+  }catch(error){return pending([400,401,403,404,405,413,429].includes(error?.status)?error.status:503);}
+  finally{parent?.finish();}
+ }});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

## lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,100 @@
+import {readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-preparation.mjs';
+import {retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+// Internal fixed wire vocabulary. DATA never represents approval or admission.
+export const finiteNativeOrigin='https://delegated-canary.agentfriendlyweb.dev';
+export const finiteNativeOriginalUrl=finiteNativeOrigin+'/connections/finite-prepare';
+export const finiteNativeTarget='https://afw-private.invalid/_finite-owner/choice';
+export const finiteNativeActor='afw-own-successor-closure-v1';
+export const finiteNativeHeaders=['x-afw-finite-boundary','x-afw-finite-original-method','x-afw-finite-original-url','x-afw-finite-version','x-afw-finite-request-id'];
+export const finiteNativePreservedHeaders=['Cf-Access-Jwt-Assertion','Origin','Cookie','Content-Type'];
+export const finiteNativeUuid=value=>typeof value==='string'&&/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[1-5][a-f0-9]{3}-[89ab][a-f0-9]{3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(value);
+export function finiteNativeFailure(status=503){const error=Error('Finite native conduit unavailable');error.status=status;throw error;}
+export function finiteNativePending(status=503){return Response.json({status:'admin_pending',mayRetry:false,operationalReadiness:false},{status,headers:{'Cache-Control':'no-store'}});}
+export function finiteNativeMetadata(headers){
+ const entries=[...headers].filter(([key])=>key.startsWith('x-afw-finite-'));
+ if(entries.length!==finiteNativeHeaders.length||entries.some(([key])=>!finiteNativeHeaders.includes(key))||new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(entries)).length>2048)finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ const raw=headers.get('x-afw-finite-boundary');let boundary;
+ try{boundary=JSON.parse(raw);}catch{finiteNativeFailure(400);}
+ if(!boundary||typeof boundary!=='object'||!['startedAt','deadline','remainingMs'].every(key=>Number.isSafeInteger(boundary[key]))||JSON.stringify({startedAt:boundary.startedAt,deadline:boundary.deadline,remainingMs:boundary.remainingMs})!==raw)finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ const method=headers.get('x-afw-finite-original-method');
+ if(!['GET','POST'].includes(method)||headers.get('x-afw-finite-original-url')!==finiteNativeOriginalUrl||!finiteNativeUuid(headers.get('x-afw-finite-version'))||!finiteNativeUuid(headers.get('x-afw-finite-request-id')))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ if(method==='POST'&&headers.get('Origin')!==finiteNativeOrigin)finiteNativeFailure(403);
+ return {boundary,method};
+}
+/** Whole operation registered before any timeout/abort race. Known failure is
+ * signalled promptly, while its real cancellation remains in the retained work. */
+export async function finiteNativeOperation(lease,ctx,operation){
+ const {remainingMs}=readFiniteOwnerRequestBoundary(lease);
+ let rejectFirst,abort,timer;
+ const first=new Promise((_,reject)=>{rejectFirst=reject;});
+ const deadline=new Promise((_,reject)=>{
+  abort=()=>{try{finiteNativeFailure();}catch(error){reject(error);}};
+  lease.signal.addEventListener('abort',abort,{once:true});
+  timer=setTimeout(abort,remainingMs);
+ });
+ const actual=retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise(Promise.resolve().then(()=>{lease.assertCurrent();return operation(rejectFirst);}));
+ // waitUntil retains this same completion, not a timeout winner.
+ if(typeof ctx.waitUntil==='function')ctx.waitUntil(actual.catch(()=>{}));
+ try{return await Promise.race([actual,first,deadline]);}
+ finally{clearTimeout(timer);lease.signal.removeEventListener('abort',abort);}
+}
+/** Caller owns the actual read/failure/cancel chain until physical settlement. */
+export async function finiteNativeBytes(message,check,first,status=400){
+ const reader=message.body?.getReader();if(!reader){check();return new Uint8Array();}
+ const chunks=[];let length=0;
+ try{
+  check();
+  for(;;){const {done,value}=await reader.read();check();if(done)break;if(!(value instanceof Uint8Array)||(length+=value.length)>16384)finiteNativeFailure(status);chunks.push(value);}
+  const bytes=new Uint8Array(length);let at=0;for(const chunk of chunks){bytes.set(chunk,at);at+=chunk.length;}return bytes;
+ }catch(error){first(error);try{await reader.cancel();}catch{}throw error;}
+}
+export async function finiteNativeCancel(message,error,first){
+ first(error);try{await message.body?.cancel();}catch{}throw error;
+}
+
+// Fixed choice grammar; metadata and cookie text remain DATA, not approval.
+function choiceCookies(headers){
+ const raw=headers.get('Cookie'),assertion=headers.get('Cf-Access-Jwt-Assertion')??'';
+ if((raw?.length??0)>8192||assertion.length>8192||/[\r\n]/.test((raw??'')+assertion))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ if(raw===null)return null;
+ const cookie=raw.replace(/^[ \t]+|[ \t]+$/g,'');if(!cookie)finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ const names=new Set();let prepare=null;
+ for(const part of cookie.split('; ')){
+  const at=part.indexOf('='),name=part.slice(0,at),value=part.slice(at+1);
+  if(at<1||!/^[!#$%&'*+\-.^_\x60|~0-9A-Za-z]+$/.test(name)||names.has(name))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+  names.add(name);
+  const octets=value.startsWith('"')&&value.endsWith('"')&&value.length>=2?value.slice(1,-1):value;
+  if(!/^[\x21\x23-\x2b\x2d-\x3a\x3c-\x5b\x5d-\x7e]*$/.test(octets))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+  if(name==='__Host-afw-finite-prepare')prepare=value;
+ }
+ return prepare;
+}
+export function qualifyFiniteOwnerChoiceRequest(request,bytes){
+ if(!(request instanceof Request)||request.url!==finiteNativeOriginalUrl)finiteNativeFailure(404);
+ if(!['GET','POST'].includes(request.method))finiteNativeFailure(405);
+ if(!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ const origin=request.headers.get('Origin');
+ if(origin!==null&&origin!==finiteNativeOrigin||request.method==='POST'&&origin!==finiteNativeOrigin)finiteNativeFailure(403);
+ const cookie=choiceCookies(request.headers);
+ if(request.method==='GET'){if(bytes.length)finiteNativeFailure(400);return Object.freeze({method:'GET'});}
+ if(bytes.length>1024)finiteNativeFailure(413);
+ if(request.headers.get('Content-Type')!=='application/x-www-form-urlencoded'||!bytes.length)finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ let text;try{text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);if(/%(?![a-f0-9]{2})/i.test(text))finiteNativeFailure(400);decodeURIComponent(text.replace(/\+/g,' '));}catch{finiteNativeFailure(400);}
+ const form=new URLSearchParams(text),entries=[...form];
+ if(entries.length!==3||!['handle','projectRef','decision'].every(key=>form.getAll(key).length===1)||entries.some(([key])=>!['handle','projectRef','decision'].includes(key))||form.get('decision')!=='approve'||![form.get('handle'),form.get('projectRef')].every(value=>/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(value??'')))finiteNativeFailure(400);
+ if(cookie!==form.get('handle'))finiteNativeFailure(403);
+ return Object.freeze({method:'POST',handle:form.get('handle'),projectRef:form.get('projectRef')});
+}
+export function finiteNativeChoiceHtml(response,bytes){
+ if(!(response instanceof Response)||response.status!==200||!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)||bytes.length>16384||!/^text\/html(?:;|$)/i.test(response.headers.get('content-type')??''))finiteNativeFailure();
+ try{new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);}catch{finiteNativeFailure();}
+ const headers=new Headers();let size=0;
+ for(const [key,value]of response.headers){
+  if(!['content-type','content-security-policy','referrer-policy','set-cookie','cache-control','x-frame-options','x-content-type-options'].includes(key)||value.length>8192||(size+=key.length+value.length)>16384)finiteNativeFailure();
+  if(key==='x-content-type-options'&&value!=='nosniff')finiteNativeFailure();
+  headers.append(key,value);
+ }
+ headers.set('Content-Type','text/html; charset=utf-8');headers.set('Cache-Control','no-store');headers.set('Referrer-Policy','no-referrer');headers.set('X-Content-Type-Options','nosniff');headers.set('X-Frame-Options','DENY');
+ headers.set('Content-Security-Policy',"default-src 'none'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'");
+ return new Response(bytes,{status:200,headers});
+}
~~~~~~~~~~

~~~~~~~~~~diff
diff --git a/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-notice-consent-body.mjs b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-notice-consent-body.mjs
--- /dev/null
+++ b/lib/mcp-qa-finite-owner-notice-consent-body.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+import {retainFiniteOwnerStagePromise as retain} from './mcp-qa-finite-stage-work.mjs';
+import {finiteNativeFailure as fail} from './mcp-qa-finite-owner-native-wire.mjs';
+// Retain actual read and cancellation, including work after the HTTP race fails.
+export async function readFiniteOwnerNoticeConsentBody(message,check,firstFailure){
+ const reader=message.body?.getReader();if(!reader){check();return new Uint8Array();}
+ const chunks=[];let size=0;
+ try{
+  check();
+  for(;;){
+   const {done,value}=await retain(reader.read());check();if(done)break;
+   if(!(value instanceof Uint8Array)||(size+=value.length)>1024)fail(413);
+   chunks.push(value);
+  }
+  const bytes=new Uint8Array(size);let offset=0;for(const chunk of chunks){bytes.set(chunk,offset);offset+=chunk.length;}
+  new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);check();return bytes;
+ }catch(error){firstFailure(error);try{await retain(reader.cancel());}catch{}throw error;}
+}
~~~~~~~~~~
